paṉipaṭu cōlai vēṅkaṭat tumpar
 moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum nalkuvar 	(akam. 211 : 7-8)
eṉṉum kaṭaikkaḻakac ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷ. tamiḻa vaṇikariṉ vaṭaticai moḻipeyar tēyac celavaik kūṟutal kāṇka. teluṅkeṉṉum vaṭuku mutaṟkālattil tamiḻi ṉiṉṟum mika vēṟupaṭṭirukkamuṭiyātu. vēṅkaṭat tumpar moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟatu vaṭukanāṭṭaiyē eṉpatu teḷivuṟu tēṟṟam. tām eṉpatu tāmu eṉṟum, tam eṉpatu tama eṉṟum, tampiṉ eṉpatu tammuṭu eṉṟum, il eṉpatu illu eṉṟum, iṉṟum teluṅkil vaḻaṅkuvatāl, tammil allatu tamil eṉṉum kūṭṭuc col akkāla vaṭukiṟku muṟṟum iyalpāṉatē.
alarmēlmaṅkai eṉṉum toṭarccol piṉṉar alarmēl eṉṟu kuṟuki vaḻaṅkuvatupōl, tamilmoḻi eṉpatum nāḷaṭaivil tamil eṉṟu kuṟuki vaḻaṅkutal iyalpē. lakaratti ṉiṉṟē ḷakaramum ḷakarattiṉiṉṟē ḻakaramum tōṉṟi yiruttalāl, lakaram nēraṭiyākavō ḷakara vāyilākavō ḻakaramāyt tiritaluṇṭu.
e-ṭu:	māl - maḻai, ēlā - ēḻā (paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ 
	maṉaiviyai viḷikkum col), kal - kaḷ - kāḷ - 
	kāḻ (karuppu) nālikai (mūṅkil) - nāḷam 
	(uṭṭuḷai) - nāḻi (uṭṭuḷaip paṭi)
ḻakaram lakarattiṉ mikap pintiyatātaliṉ, tamil eṉṉum lakara vīṟṟu vaṭivam ap peyariṉ toṉmaiyaiyum uṇarttum.
iṉi, taṉmāṉam tamar eṉṉum coṟkaḷil, taṉ tam eṉpaṉa paṭarkkai cuṭṭātu conta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutal pōl, tamil eṉṉum collilum tam eṉpatu conta eṉṟu poruḷ paṭumāṟu, tamiḻarē ap peyarait tam moḻikku iṭṭukkoṇ ṭaṉar eṉak koḷḷalum oṉṟu. cāyuṅkālam eṉpatu cāyaṅkālam eṉṟu tirintapiṉ, cāyam eṉṉum peyareccamē vaṭamoḻiyil cāyuṅ kālattaik kuṟittal pōl; tamil eṉṉum peyaraṭai peyart taṉmaippaṭṭu moḻiyaik kuṟittateṉka. t.saha«-ï.sah«.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻ eṉṉum peyarukkuk kūṟappaṭṭa poruṭkaraṇiyaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟuḷḷum, taṉiyāka ḻakarattaiyuṭaiyatu, tannāṭṭu moḻi, eṉṉum iraṇṭē poruttamāṉavai yeṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉum, ciṟantatu tōṉṟum varai ivaiyē koḷḷat takkaṉa veṉṟum eṇṇik koḷka.
vaṭuku koṭuntamiḻnilai kaṭantu moḻinilai yaṭainta piṉ, vaṭukar(teluṅkar) tamiḻukkiṭṭa peyar aruvam eṉpatu. atu aravam eṉat tirintatu. centamiḻ nāṭṭaic cērnta koṭuntamiḻ nāṭukaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭeṉat tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  piṟkālattil centamiḻ nilapparappu mikac curuṅkiviṭṭa taṉāl, ataṟkēṟpak koṭuntamiḻ nilaṅkaḷum paṇṭaic centamiḻ nilattiṟ kuḷḷēyē aṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa. an nilaimaiyaiyē,
teṉpāṇṭi kuṭṭam kuṭamkaṟkā vēṇpūḻi
 paṉṟi aruvā ataṉvaṭakku - naṉṟāya
 cīta malāṭu puṉaṉāṭu centamiḻcēr
 ētamil paṉṉirunāṭ ṭeṇ
eṉṉu miṭaikkāla veṇpāk kāṭṭum.
`aruvā ataṉ vaṭakku' eṉṟatu, aruvā nāṭṭaiyum aruvā vaṭatalai nāṭṭaiyum. ivai tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭa kōṭiyil teluṅka nāṭṭai aṭuttiruntaṉa. ataṉāl nāṭupaṟṟit tamiḻarai aruvar eṉa aḻaittaṉar teluṅkar. ellaippuṟap pakutip peyarai nāṭu muḻuvataṟkum iṭuvatu ayalār iyalpu. cintuveḷi paṟṟi nāvalan tēyattaip pāracīkar hintu eṉṟum kirēkkar intōs eṉṟum kuṟittataiyum; mukalāya aracar kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭōṭu tamiḻnāṭṭaiyuñ cērttuk karu nāṭakam eṉa aḻaittatai yum, nōkkuka.
aruvar aruvar eṉāvi ṟaiñciṉar
 apayam apayam eṉāna ṭuṅkiyē
eṉpatu kaliṅkattup paraṇi.
oruva roruvarmēl vīḻntuvaṭa nāṭar
 aruvar aruvareṉa añci - veruvantu
 tīttīttī eṉṟayarvar ceṉṉi paṭaivīrar
 pōrkkaliṅka mīteḻunta pōtu
eṉpatu paḻaiya veṇpā.
aruvar moḻi aruvam.
karunaṭar (kaṉṉaṭar) tamiḻar eṉṉuñ collait tikuḷar eṉat tirittu vaḻaṅkuvar. vaṭamoḻi teṉṉāṭu vantapiṉ, tamiḻai ataṉoṭu oppunōkkit teṉmoḻi eṉṟaṉar.
tiraviṭam, eṉpatu tamiḻam eṉpataṉ tiripeṉṟu muṉṉarē viḷakkappeṟṟatu. tiraviṭam, teṉmoḻi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, tiraviṭa moḻika ḷellāvaṟṟaiyum taṉṉuḷ aṭakkik koṇ ṭirunta tamiḻiṉ paṇṭait talaimaiyai uṇarttum.
11. mūvēntar peyar
talaik kaḻakak kālattiṟku muṉpē, cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar nāvalam poḻil eṉṉum intu tēyattai āḷat toṭaṅki viṭṭataṉāl, am mūvēntar kuṭit tōṟṟamum varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmaittākum. itaṉālēyē, tirukkuṟaḷai vaṭanūl vaḻiyākak kāṭṭiya parimēlaḻakarum, `paḻaṅkuṭi' eṉpataṟkut toṉṟutoṭṭu varukiṉṟa kuṭi eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, toṉṟutoṭṭu varutal cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya reṉṟāṟ pōlap paṭaippuk kālan toṭaṅki mēmpaṭṭu varutal eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār.
mutaṟkālattil nāvalam poḻil muḻutum mūvēntar āṭcik kuṭpaṭṭiruntamai,
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmalai yaṭukkattuk 
 kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
 vaṭaticaik kaṅkaiyum imayamum koṇṭu
 teṉṟicai yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi! 
(cilap. nāṭukāṇ kātai, 19-22)
irunila maruṅkiṟ porunaraip peṟāac
 ceruveṅ kātaliṉ tirumā vaḷavaṉ
 ............................   
 puṇṇiyat ticaimukam pōkiya annāḷ
 acaivil ūkkattu nacaipiṟak koḻiyap
 pakaivilak kiyatip payaṅkeḻu malaiyeṉa
 imaiyavar uṟaiyuñ cimaiyap piṭarttalaik
 koṭuvari yoṟṟik koḷkaiyiṟ peyarvōṉ
(cilap. intira viḻavūreṭutta kātai, 88-97)
kumariyoṭu vaṭavimayattu orumoḻi vaittu ulakāṇṭa cēralātaṟkut tikaḻoḷi ñāyiṟṟuc cōḻaṉ makaḷ īṉṟa maintaṉ koṅkar ceṅkaḷam vēṭṭuk kaṅkaip pēryāṟṟuk karai pōkiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ
(cilap. vāḻttuk kātai, uraippāṭṭu maṭai)
eṉṉum pakutikaḷāl uyttuṇarappaṭum. āriyar varumuṉ vaṭanāṭu muḻutum tiraviṭar paraviyiruntamaiyum, cila vaṭanāṭṭūrp peyar tamiḻāyiruttalum itai valiyuṟuttam.
teṟkē muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭam muḻutum paḻam pāṇṭi nāṭē. atu ēṟattāḻa īrāyiram kal tolaivu nīṇṭatu. itēyaḷavu vaṭakkil kīḻppakuti cōḻanāṭākavum mēṟpakuti cēranāṭākavum iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. kaṇṇaṉ aḻitta tākac collappaṭum cōṇitapuram paḻañ cēranāṭṭaic cērntatē. atu māvali eṉṉum cēra māvēntaṉiṉ makaṉ vāṇaṉatu talainakar.
tamiḻa vēntar eṉṟum mūvarēyātaliṉ, avar muc cuṭaraiyum tam kulamutalākak kūṟikkoṇṭaṉar. pāṇṭiyaṉ tiṅkaṭkulamum, cōḻaṉ katiravak kulamum, cēraṉ neruppuk kulamum āvar. pāratattiṟ collappaṭum, tiṅkaḷ marapākiya parata kulam paḻam pāṇṭiyak kiḷaiyum, irāmāyaṇattiṟ collappaṭum katiravak kulam(cūriya vamicam) paḻañ cōḻak kiḷaiyum ākum. mucukuntaṉ, māntātā, cipi mutaliyōr teṉṉāṭṭuc cōḻar kuṭikkum vaṭanāṭṭuk katiravak kulattiṟkum potu muṉṉōrākac collap paṭutal, ivviru kuṭikaḷum orē kulattaic cērntamaiyālēyē. cipiyiṉ vaḻiyiṉaṉ ātalālēyē cōḻaṉ cempiyaṉ eṉap paṭṭāṉ. piṟkālattut teluṅkac cōṭar(cōḻar) moḻipaṟṟip pirintu pōṉatu pōṉṟē, muṟkālattuk katiravakkula vaṭavaracarum moḻiyum cēymaiyumpaṟṟip pirintu pōyiṉar eṉka.
nārataṉai mūvulaku mulāvi(tirilōka cañcāri) eṉṟatu cēra cōḻapāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷpaṟṟiyē. ulakam eṉpatu mutalāku peyarāy nāṭṭaiyum kuṟikkum.
ittuṇaip paḻaimai vāynta cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyak kuṭip  peyarkaḷ, tamiḻāyaṉṟi vēṟu moḻiyāyiruttal muṭi yātu. āyiṉum, āriya veṟiyar intiya nākarikattai āriyamākak kāṭṭalvēṇṭi, mūvēntar kuṭippeyaraiyum vaṭanāṭṭut toṭarpukoṇṭa vaṭacollākak kāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ
pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpatu mūvēntaruḷḷum muntiyavaṉ peyar. paḻam pāṇṭiya nāṭē tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam. kumarikkaṇṭam teṉ kōṭiyi liruntatiṉālēyē, atai yāṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ teṉ ṉavaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpatu paṇṭu eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu tirinta teṉṟum, paḻaimaiyāṉavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ koṇṭa teṉṟum, colvatuṇṭu. pāṇṭiyaṉ mūvēntaruḷ maṭṭu maṉṟip piṟavaracarai nōkkiyum paḻaimaiyāṉavaṉē. mōkūrt talaivaṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ paṭaittalaivaṉumākiya oru ciṟṟaracaṉum paḻaiyaṉ eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟiruntāṉ. āyiṉum, ip poruṭkaraṇiyam attuṇaip porutta māyt tōṉṟa villai.
pāṇṭi eṉpatu kāḷaiyaik kuṟikkuñ col. atu pāṇṭil, pāṇṭiyam eṉṉum vaṭivuṅkoḷḷum. oru maṟavaṉaik kāḷai eṉpatu marapu. ataṉāl. atu cilarkku iyaṟpeyarākavum iṭappaṭum. valimai, maṟam, uḻaippu, poṟuppu mutaliya arun tiṟaṅkaḷ vāynta kāḷai pōṉṟavaṉaik kāḷaiyeṉṟal uvamaiyāku peyar. oru nāṭṭaik kākkum aracaṉukku ik kuṇaṅkaḷ iṉṟiyamai yātavai. ātalāl, poruṭpālil araciyaṟ pakutiyil iṭukkaṇaḻiyāmai eṉṉum atikārattil,
maṭuttavā yellām pakaṭaṉṉā ṉuṟṟa
 iṭukkaṇ iṭarppā ṭuṭaittu 	(kuṟaḷ. 624)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
kāḷai eṉpatu āṇmakaṉ, kaṭṭiḷamaiyōṉ, pālai nilat talaivaṉ ākiya mūvaraiyuṅ kuṟikkumeṉṟu tivākaram kūṟum. ātalāl, kāḷaiyaik kuṟikkum pāṇṭi eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟē, pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum.
aruccuṉaṉ tirunīrāṭṭiṟkut teṉṉāṭu vantapōtu cittirāṅkataṉ eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷai maṇantā ṉeṉṉum katai paṟṟi, pāṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyar tirintateṉṟu, varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmai vāynta pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṭippeyaraik ki.mu. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭi ṉaṉāṉa pāṇṭuvoṭu toṭarpupaṭuttuvatu, viṇṇaka mīṉaiyum, maṇṇaka māṉaiyum oṉṟā yiṇaippatu pōṉṟatē. aruccuṉaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ makaḷai maṇakku muṉpē, avaṉ māmaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉap peyar peṟṟiruntamaiyai, ak kataiyē kūṟukiṉṟatē! iṉi, aiñciṟṟacarar tuṇaikkoṇṭu pāṇṭiyaṉ āṇṭatiṉāṟ peṟṟa pañcavaṉ eṉṉum peyarum, pāṇṭavaraik kuṟikkum pañcavar eṉpataṉōṭu toṭarpuṭaiyataṉṟu. pāṇṭavaraik kuṟikkum col eṉṟum paṉmai vaṭivilēyē niṟkum. ākavē, añcavaṉ eṉpatē pañcavaṉ eṉṟu āriyarāl tirikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. pañcavar eṉṟum aivarē. pañcavaṉē aivarōṭu cērnta āṟāmavaṉ.
pāṇṭi eṉṉuñ colliṉ vērpporuḷ vaṭṭam eṉpatē. vaṭṭam eṉpatu uruṭciyaiyum tiraṭciyaiyum kuṟikkum. kāḷai uruṇṭu tiraṇṭiruppatu. atu kuṇṭā yiruppatāl kuṇṭai yeṉṟum, viṭaittiruppatāl viṭai yeṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka. viṭaittal - paruttal.
cōḻaṉ
cōḻanāṭu nīrvaḷattiṟkum nelvaḷattiṟkum aṉṟum iṉṟum peyar peṟṟatu. puṉalnāṭu, vaḷanāṭu eṉṉum nāṭṭup peyarkaḷum, vaḷavaṉ eṉṉum cōḻaṉ kuṭip peyarum,
vēḻa muṭaittu malaināṭu mētakka
 cōḻa vaḷanāṭu cōṟuṭaittu - pūḻiyarkōṉ
 teṉṉāṭu muttuṭaittu teṇṇīr vayaṟṟoṇṭai
 naṉṉāṭu cāṉṟō ruṭaittu
eṉṉum auvaiyār pāṭṭum, tañcai māvaṭṭattaik kuṟikkum tamiḻnāṭṭuk kaḷañciyam eṉṉum iṟṟaic collum, itait teḷivāyk kāṭṭum.
cōṟeṉṟu ciṟappākac colvatu aricic cōṟṟaiyē. arici nelliṉ uḷḷīṭātalāl, nel eṉpatum ciṟupāṉmai ariciyaik kuṟikkum. neṟcōṟu, neṟparukkai, neṟkañci, neṟ pori mutaliya vaḻakkukaḷaik kāṇka. nelliṟkuc col eṉṟoru peyaruṇṭu. ataṉiṉṟē cōṟu, coṉṟi eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ piṟakkum.
o. nō:
nal-naṉṟi, pal-paṉṟi.
(tel - teṉ - teṉpu - tempu - teḷivu)
tel - teḷ - teṟu - tēṟu = teḷivu.
teṟu - teṟṟu - teṟṟeṉa = teḷivāka
cul - cuṟu - cūṟu. culavutal = cuṟṟutal, 
cuḻalutal.
cūṟutal = cūḻtal. cuṟu - cuṟṟu.
cōḻanāṭu nelliṟku allatu cōṟṟiṟkuc ciṟanta tiṉāl, nellaik kuṟikkum col eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu ap peyar peṟṟirukkalām.
col - (cōḷ) - cōḻam - cōḻaṉ.
o.nō: kal - kaḷ - kāḷ - kāḻ - kāḻakam = karumai. kil - kīḻ,
kel - kēḻal. cul (vaḷai) - cūḻ. tul (poruntu) - tōḻam-tōḻaṉ. pul (tuḷai) - pūḻai. pol, poḷ - pōḻ.
cōḷappeyariṉiṉṟu pirittukkāṭṭavum cōḻam eṉṉum vaṭivu vēṇṭap peṟum.
cōḻanāṭu, mutaṟkālattil  nel mikutiyāy viḷaikkap paṭṭatu maṭṭumaṉṟit tāṉāy viḷainta nilamākavum iruntiruk kalām. māntarāl viḷaikkappaṭum payirkaḷellām, muṉpu tāmāy viḷaintavaiyē.
nilaittiṇaiyāl (tāvarattāl) oru nāṭu peyar peṟuvatu iyalpē. ēḻu tīvukaḷuḷ, nāvalantīvu, iṟalittīvu, ilavantīvu, kucaittīvu, tēkkantīvu eṉṉum aintum nilait tiṇaiyāṟ peyar peṟṟavai. muḻukippōṉa kumari kaṇṭattilum ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻ kuṟumpaṉai nāṭum iruntamai kāṇka.
cēraṉ
cēranāṭu malaiyāṟ ciṟantatu. ataṉāl poṟaiyaṉ, malaiyaṉ, malaiyamāṉ, malaināṭaṉ eṉpaṉa cēraṉ peyar kaḷākum. tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa ciṟanta malait toṭarkaḷ mēṟkut toṭarcci malaiyum kiḻakkut toṭarcci malaiyumē. ivaṟṟaik koṇṭatu cēranāṭu. an nāṭṭu nilamuḻutum malaiyiṉ irupakkamu muḷḷa cāralē. cāralāvatu malaiccarivu nilam. ataṉāl cāral nāṭaṉ eṉpatu malaināṭaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkum.
vēral vēli vērkkōṭ palaviṉ
 cāral nāṭa cevviyai yākumati 	(kuṟun. 18 : 1-2)
eṉṉum kuṟuntokaic ceyyuṭ pakutiyil  iravukkuṟi vantu nīṅkum talaimakaṉ cāral nāṭa eṉat tōḻiyāl viḷikkap paṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
cāral eṉpatu aṉṉīṟu peṟṟuc cāralaṉ eṉṟākum. atu piṉṉarc cēralaṉ eṉat tirintu kuṭamalaināṭṭu vēntaṉaik kuṟittatu. cēralaṉ eṉpatu īṟṟayal tokkuc cēraṉ eṉṟāyatu. cēraṉ eṉpatum, ceyvaṉ eṉpatu ceyval eṉṟu tirintāṟpōl īṟu tirintu cēral eṉṟāyiṟṟu. māṉ eṉṉum īṟu cēriṉ, cēraṉ eṉpatu cēramāṉ eṉṟām. māṉ eṉpatu makaṉ eṉpataṉ marūu.
tamiḻmakkaḷ kumarikkaṇṭa aḷavilēyē vāḻntapōtu pāṇṭiyaṉ oruvaṉē āṇṭāṉeṉṟum piṉpu makkaḷ peruki vaṭakkiṟ paraviyapiṉ oru pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ iḷavalār iruvar cōḻaṉum cēraṉumāki vaṭanāvalai āṇṭā reṉṟum, oru cevimarapuc ceyti vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu. mūvēntaruḷ muntiya pāṇṭiyaṉai āriyaṉākkiviṭiṉ, ēṉaiyiruvarum tāmāka āriyarāyviṭuvar eṉpatu tamiḻp pakaivar karuttu. av vēmāṟṟu ārāycciyum urimai yuṇarcciyum mikka ikkālattuc cellā tākum.
vaṭaticaik kaṅkaiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu,
 teṉṟicai yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi	(cilap. 11 : 21-2)
eṉpatu mūvēntaruḷḷum pāṇṭiyaṉ muntiyavaṉ eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟām.
12. paḻantamiḻaka iṭappeyarkaḷ
kumari
kumari eṉpatu tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamāṉa paḻam pāṇṭiyiṉ teṉ kōṭiyaṭutta oru māperu malaittoṭariṉ peyarum, ak kaṇṭattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyaṭutta oru pērāṟṟiṉ peyaru māyiruntatu. ip peyaraiyum, kumariyāṟṟiṉ maṟupeyarāṉa kaṉṉi eṉpataiyum, kumarimalaiyiṟ piṟantōṭiya paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅ karaiyilirunta talaikkaḻaka irukkaiyāṉa pāṇṭiyar mutal talainakariṉ maturai eṉṉum peyaraiyum, vaṭa collākak kāṭṭiviṭiṉ, tamiḻ vaṭamoḻikkup piṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum, intiya nākarikam āriya nākarikam eṉṟum, āyviṭum eṉṉum karuttuṭaṉ, tamiḻp pakai varāṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷar toṉṟutoṭṭu ak kumarik kaṇṭa iṭappeyarkaṭku vaṭa coṉmūlaṅ kaṟpittu varalāyiṉar.
kumari eṉpatu kum eṉṉum tūya tamiḻ mutaṉilai yiṉiṉṟu piṟanta tamiḻccol.
kummutal = kuvital, kūṭutal, tiraḷutal, paruttal.
kum - kummal - kummali = paruttavaḷ. kum - kumukku = kūṭṭam.
kum - kumar = kūṭaṟkēṟṟa iḷamai allatu parumai, ataiyuṭaiya 		kaṉṉi, kaṉṉimai, iḷamai, aḻiyāt taṉmai.
kumar - kumaraṉ = kūṭaṟkēṟṟa iḷaiñaṉ, iḷañaṉāṉa murukaṉ. 
o. nō: muruku = iḷamai, murukaṉ. muruku - murukaṉ.
kumar - kumari = iḷaiñai, kaṉṉi, kaṉṉiyākak karutappeṟum  		kāḷi, ōr āṟu, oru malai.
kūṭaṟkēṟṟa nilaiyil ellā vuyiriṉaṅkaḷum atataṟ kēṟṟavāṟu paruttiruttal iyalpu. itaṉālēyē, āṭu māṭu mutaliya cila vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷum kōḻi mayil mutaliya paṟavai yiṉaṅkaḷum, parutta iḷamainilaiyil viṭaiyeṉappaṭum. viṭaittal = paruttal. viṭai - viṭalai = iḷaiñaṉ, pālainilat talaivaṉ. virgin eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟkum, virgo, to swell eṉṟu poruṭkaraṇiyaṅkāṭṭuvar. oruvaṉaik kumaraṉ eṉṟum oruttiyaik kumari eṉṟum collumpōtu, iḷamaik karuttuṭaṉ parumai allatu valimaik karuttum kalantiruttalai nōkkuka.
vēṉalaṅ kiḻavaṉoṭu veṅkatir vēntaṉ
 tāṉalan tirukat taṉmaiyil kuṉṟi
 mullaiyum kuṟiñciyum muṟaimaiyil tirintu
 nalliyal piḻantu naṭuṅkutuya ruṟuttup
 pālai eṉpatōr paṭivaṅ koḷḷum 	(cilap.11: 62-6)
kumari eṉṉuṅ kāḷi toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭup pālai nilat teyvam. pālai eṉpatu mullaiyuṅ kuṟiñciyum mutuvēṉilil aṭaiyum vaṟaṇṭa nilai. kumarimalai kuṟiñcinilat taṉmaiyāl ap peyar peṟa urimaiyuḷḷatu. kāḷi pōril veṟṟi tarum koṟṟavai yeṉṟum, kuru eṉṉum koppuḷa nōy varuvikkum ammai yeṉṟum, karutap paṭṭapiṉ, avaḷ vaḻipāṭu ēṉai nilaṅkaṭkum potuvāyiṟṟu. vaṭanāṭṭilum tamiḻar vaḻiyiṉarē perumpālarā yirukkiṉṟamaiyiṉ, aṅkum kāḷi vaṇakkam iruntu varukiṉṟatu. varalāṟaṟiyātavar vaṭakkiliruppa tellām āriya vaḻakkameṉat tavaṟākak karutik koṇṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. kumari tamiḻar potut teyvamāṉa piṉ, avaḷ peyar ōr āṟṟiṟkum iṭappaṭṭatu.
kumaraṉ, kumari eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil kumāra, kumāri eṉa nīṇṭu, muṟaiyē makaṉaiyum makaḷaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. vaṭavar kumāra eṉṉum collaik ku+māra eṉṟu pirittu, cāvillātavaṉ. ataṉāl iḷamaiyāṉavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi vaṭacollākak kāṭṭuvar. āṟiluñ cāvu, nūṟiluñ cāvu. ātalāl, aṅṅaṉam poruḷ kūṟuvatu poruntātu. kumari eṉṉum coṟku uṭal tiraṇṭa iḷaiñai eṉpatē iyaṟporu ḷeṉpatu, kōṭic cēlaikkoru veḷḷai, kumarip peṇṇuk koru piḷḷai  eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyāl teriyavarum. iḷamai paṟṟiyē īṉāvāḻai kumarivāḻai eṉap paṭutalum eṉka.
kaṉṉi
muḻukip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyil kumari yeṉṟoru pēriyāṟiruntamai, muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu.
vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
eṉṟu tolkāppiyac ciṟappup pāyirattilum,
teṉāa turukeḻu kumari	(puṟam. 6)
eṉṟu puṟappāṭṭilum kuṟikkappaṭṭatu kumariyāṟē.
kumari, kaṉṉi eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ coṟkaḷātalāl, kumariyāṟu kaṉṉiyāṟu eṉavumpaṭum.
maṉṉu mālai veṇkuṭaiyāṉ
vaḷaiyāc ceṅkō latuvōccik
 kaṉṉi taṉṉaip puṇarntālum
pulavāy vāḻi kāvēri                               
eṉṉum cilappatikārak kāṉalvarip pāṭal, kumariyāṟṟaik kaṉṉi yeṉak kuṟittal kāṇka. kaṉṉi eṉpatu kāḷiyiṉ peyarē.
kaṉṉi eṉṉum col, makkaḷiṉattup peṇṇaik kuṟikkum pōtu, pūppaṭaintu maṇamākāta peṇṇaiyē kuṟikkum.
oru peṇ vāḻnāḷ muḻutum maṇamākātirukkalā mātalāl, iḷaṅkaṉṉi kaṉṉikai yeṉappaṭuvāḷ. kai eṉpatu oru ciṟumaip poruḷ piṉṉoṭṭu (Diminutive suffix).
e-ṭu:	kuṭi (vīṭu) - kuṭikai (ciṟuvīṭu) - kuṭicai.
pūppaṭaiyāta ciṟumiyaiyum maṇamāṉa peṇṇaiyum kaṉṉi yeṉṟu collum vaḻakkamillai. kaṉṉikaḻital kaṉṉi yaḻital eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka.
kaṉṉi eṉṉum collaik kaṉyā eṉṟum, kaṉṉikai eṉṉum collaik kaṉyakā eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar tirittuc ciṟumi, makaḷ eṉṟa poruḷkaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvar. ataṟkēṟpa, kaṉ (tikaḻ), kaṉa (ciṟu) eṉpavaṟṟai vērākak kāṭṭuvar.
tikaḻtalaik kuṟikkum kaṉ eṉṉuñ col valintu poruttuvatākum. ciṟumaiyaik kuṟikkum kaṉa eṉṉuñ col pūppaṭainta peṇṇiṟkup poruntātu.
iṉi, vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vērākak kāṭṭum kaṉ, kaṉa eṉṉum iru collum teṉcol tiripē.
kal - kaṉṟu. kaṉṟutal = erital, karukutal,ciṉattal.
kal - kaṉ - kaṉal = neruppu. kaṉalutal = erital, ciṉattal.
kaṉali = katiravaṉ.
kal - kaḷ - kāḷ = kāḷam (kāḷavāyil) = cuṇṇāmpuk kal nīṟṟum cuḷḷai. kāḷ-kāy. kāytal = erital, ciṉattal, tikaḻtal
kaḷ - kaṇ - kaṇa - kaṇappu=neruppu. tikaḻtal neruppiṉ 	toḻil. kaṇ - kaṇai = cūṭu. kaṉ - va. kaṉ.
kul - kuṉ- kuṉṉi = mikac ciṟiyatu. kuṉṉutal = ciṟuttal, 	oṭuṅkutal.
kul - kuṉṟu - kuṉṟi. kuṉṟu - kuṉṟam.
kul - kuḷ - kuḷḷam, kuḷḷai. kuḷ - kuṭṭai. kuḷ - kuṟu- kuṟṟi -kucci. kuṟu - kuṟuku. kuṟu - kuṟaḷ - kuṟaḷi.
mikac ciṟiyavaṟṟai naṉṉiyuṅ kuṉṉiyum eṉpatu nellai vaḻakku.
kuṉ-kaṉ-kaṉa(va.)
kaṉṉutal eṉpatu paḻuttalaik kuṟikkum ōr aruntamiḻc col. veppattiṉālāvatu aḻuttattiṉālāvatu uḷḷaṅ kaiyilum uḷḷaṅkālilum arattaṅkaṭṭic civantuviṭṭāl, arattaṅ kaṉṉi viṭṭatu eṉpar. kaṉṉutal vēṟu; kaṉṟutal vēṟu. muṉṉatu paḻuttal; piṉṉatu valuttal. kaṉi (paḻam) eṉṉum col kaṉṉi (paḻuttatu) eṉpataṉ tokuttalē.
o.nō: kiḷḷi - kiḷi, maṇṇi - maṇi.
kiḷḷutalāvatu kāykaṉikaḷaik kottutal. maṇṇu talāvatu kallai mācaṟak kaḻuvutal. kiḷappatu kiḷi eṉpatu ārāycciyillār kūṟṟeṉa aṟika.
kaḷḷutal eṉṉum viṉai vaḻakkaṟṟapiṉ, kaḷi eṉṉum viṉaip peyar allatu viṉaimutaṟpeyar mutaṉilaiyāy vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē, kaṉi eṉṉum viṉaimutaṟpeyarum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉa aṟika.
pūppu eṉṉum coṟ pōṉṟē kaṉṉutal eṉṉum collum nilaittiṇaik kuriyatāyiruppatum, (mature) eṉṉum āṅkilac collum paḻuttal pūppaṭaital eṉṉum iruporuḷilum vaḻaṅkuvatum iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkaṉa.
kaṉinta kaṉipōṉṟa pūppaṭainta peṇṇum nukarccik kēṟṟavaḷ eṉpataiyē, kaṉṉi eṉṉum col uṇarttum camaintavaḷ(pakkuva māṉavaḷ) eṉṉuñ collaiyum nōkkuka.
maturai
maturaiyeṉṟu mutalāvatu peyar peṟṟatu paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟaṅ karait talaikkaḻaka irukkaiyē. atuvum iṭaikkaḻaka irukkai yākiya kapāṭapuramum(alaivāy?) muḻukip pōṉa piṉpē, iṟṟai vaikaik karai maturai amaintatu. atuvum mūṉṟām maturaiyē.
kumarikkaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tamiḻar vaḻiyiṉarē kaṇṇaṉ maturaiyaiyum amaittaṉar. ataṟku ap peyar iṭṭatu tam muṉṉōraiyum avar nakaraiyum niṉaivukūrtaṟkē. ap peyar annāṭṭu moḻiyiyalpiṟkēṟpa maturā eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. kaṇṇaṉ kālamākiya pāratak kālattil vaikai maturaiyiṉmaiyāl, nāvalan tēyattil iraṇṭāvatu ēṟpaṭṭa maturai vaṭanāṭṭu maturaiyē. ātalāl, atai amaittavar talaikkaḻaka mirunta nilappakutiyai muḻukkiya mutaṟ kaṭal kōḷiṉiṉṟu tappiyavarāyiruttal vēṇṭum. vaikai maturai pāratak kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭatākum. ataṟkum pintiyavai māṉavīraṉ  maturaiyum tamiḻnāṭṭu vaṭamaturaiyum eṉka. vaṭanāṭṭu maturai nōkkiyē talaikkaḻaka maturai teṉmaturai yeṉap paṭṭateṉa aṟika.
maturai eṉṉum iṭappeyar, tiṅkaḷaik kuṟikkum mati eṉṉum colliliruntu tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. talaikkaḻaka maturai yamaitta pāṇṭiyaṉ, taṉ kulamutalākak koṇṭa cuṭarppeyarai aṭiyākakkoṇṭu, matirai eṉṟu an nakarkkup peyariṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. kuti eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu kutirai eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiyiruttal kāṇka. matirai eṉpatu piṉpu olippeḷimaipaṟṟi maturai eṉat tirintuḷḷatu.
o: nō: etirkai - etukai.
kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ vēṇṭukōṭkiṇaṅki, civa peru māṉ taṉ caṭaimuṭiyiluḷḷa matiyiṉiṉṟu matuvaip poḻinta iṭam maturai yeṉappaṭṭateṉṟu, tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu tolkatai muṟaippaṭṭa uṉṉippuc colliyal (Guessing Etymology) eṉṟu kūṟiviṭukka.
ticaiticai tēṉārkkun tirumaruta muṉṟuṟai 	(kalit.26)
vaikaik karaiyaic cārntatātaliṉ, maruta muṉṟuṟai eṉpatu paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟaṅkarai maturaip peyarukkuk karaṇiya mākātu.
1
ōṅka liṭaivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
 ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
 miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉaiyatu
 taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ. 	(taṇṭiyalaṅkāra vuraimēṟkōḷ)
iyaṉilaip paṭalam
(tōrā. ki. mu. 50,000-10,000)
1. muṟpaṭai
i kumarikkaṇṭam (tōrā. ».K.?-5,500)
intumāvāri oru kālattil cantāt tīvukaḷiṉiṉṟu toṭaṅki, āciyāviṉ teṉkarai vaḻiyāy āppirikkāviṉ kīḻaikkaraimaṭṭum paraviyirunta oru nilapparappāyiruntatu. kiḷēṟṟar ip paḻam peruṅ kaṇṭattai, atil vatintirunta kuraṅkotta uyiri(pirāṇi)paṟṟi ilemuriyā (Lemuria) eṉṟaḻaik kiṉṟār. ik kaṇṭam māntaṉiṉ piṟantakamā yirukkak kūṭumāta liṉ, mika mutaṉmaiyāṉatu eṉṟār pēraṟiñar ekkēl.
oru kālattil teṉṉāppirikkāvaiyum intiyāvai yum iṇaittukkoṇṭu oru toṭarnta nilapparappiruntatu eṉṟār aṟiñar ōlṭukām.
kāṭṭu eliyaṭṭu eṉpavar eḻutiyuḷḷa maṟainta ïbyKÇah(Lost Lemuria) eṉṉum nūliluḷḷa nilappaṭatti liruntu, oru peru malaittoṭar mēlaikkaṭalil toṭaṅkit teṉvaṭalākak kumari muṉaikkut teṉpālirunta nilappakutiyil neṭuntolaivu ceṉṟu, piṉpu teṉpā lirunta nilappakutiyil neṭuntolaivu ceṉṟu, piṉpu teṉmēṟkākat tirumpi, maṭakācukkar eṉṉum āppirikkat tīvuvarai ceṉṟatākat terikiṟatu eṉṟār pērā. kā.cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷai.
kaṭalnūl (Oceanography) eṉṉum taṟkālak kalai, oru kālattil, teṉamerikkāviṉiṉṟu āppirikkāvai yoṭṭi yum intiyāvai yoṭṭiyum āttirēliyāvarai paṭarntirunta tum, `kāṇṭuvāṉāk kaṇṭam' eṉṟaṟiyappaṭṭatumāṉa, oru muḻukiya viyaṉilattaippaṟṟi, viyakkattakkavuṇmaikaḷai aṇmaiyiṟ kaṇṭupiṭittirukkiṉṟatu eṉpatu 29-07-1934-l veḷivanta intiyap paṭaviḷakkak kiḻamaiyitaḻc ceytiyākum.
kōṭiyāṇṭukaṭkumuṉ-oruvēḷai ataṟku munti-oru peruṅkaṇṭam āppirikkāvaiyum intiyāvaiyum iṇaittuk koṇṭiruntatu eṉṟār tiruvāḷar yōvāṉ iṅkilāntu.
ii kumarināṭṭu māntaṉ tōṟṟam (tōrā. ki.mu. 500,000)
ituvarai ulakiṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa paḻaiya māntaṉ elumpukkūṭukaḷuḷ, rhÈ¤ÔÉš(Java) 1891-l tūpāyicu eṉpavarāl eṭukkappaṭṭataṟkuriya nimirnta kurakku māntaṉ (Pithecanthropos Erectus) kālam ki.mu. 500,000 eṉṟu kaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 1961-l teṉṉāppirikkāvil taṅkaṉiyikkāvil ilīkki (Leakey) eṉṉum āṅkila māntaṉū laṟiñarāṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷa īrelumpuk kūṭukaḷuḷ, oṉṟaṟkuriya koṭṭaiyuṭaippāṉ (Nut-cracker Man or Sinjanthropos Boisi) iṟṟaikku 6,00,000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa vaṉ eṉṟum, iṉṉoṉṟaṟkuriya, iṉṉum peyariṭappaṭāta, naṉimika muntiya māntaṉ, kuṟainta pakkam 17,50,660 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum, kaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itai amerikka māntaṉūṟ pērāciriyar cilar maṟuttuḷḷaṉar. uṇmai eṅṅaṉa miruppiṉum, cālit tīvaiyum teṉṉāppirikkāvaiyum taṉ ṉuṭaṉ iṇaittuk koṇṭirunta nāṭē kumarikkaṇṭa mātaliṉ, an nilattu māntaṉ tōṟṟam ki.mu. 500,000 āṇṭukaṭku muntiya teṉṟu maṟuppacca miṉṟik kūṟalām.
iii kumari māntar moḻiyaṟṟa nilai (tōrā. ki.mu. 5,00,000 - 1,00,000)
kumarināṭṭu māntaṉ, mutaṟkālattil nilaiyāṉa maṇavuṟa viṉṟi vilaṅkupōl avvappōtu taṉ vēṭkai kaḷait taṇittuk koṇṭu, moḻiyum moḻiyuṇarcciyumiṉṟi iyaṟkai yāṉa uṇarcci yolikaḷaiyum viḷiyolikaḷaiyumēyuṭaiyava ṉāy, perumpālum caikaikaḷālēyē taṉ karuttai veḷip paṭutti vantāṉ. ākaiyāl, avaṉ moḻi caikai moḻiyākavē (Gesture language or Sign language) iruntatu. uṭaṟcaikai, uṟuppuc caikai eṉac caikai iru tiṟappaṭu mātalāl, mukaccaikaiyākiya valiccamum (grimace) caikaiyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
moḻiyaṟṟa nilaiyil, māntaṉ karuttum eṇṇamum uruvalippākavē (imagination) iruntuvantaṉa. uruvalippāvatu, ōr iṭattaiyō, poruḷaiyō, nikaḻcciyaiyō uḷḷattiṟ paṭam piṭittal.
moḻittuṇaiyiṉṟik karuttu nikaḻāteṉpatu ārāycci yillātār kūṟṟē. uṇarcci, vēṭkai, niṉaippu, eṇṇam, tīrmāṉam, akakkāṭci, iṉpuṟavu, pontikai (tirupti), makiḻcci, tuṉpuṟavu, ciṉam allatu veṟuppu ākiya palvēṟu uḷanikaḻcci kaḷum, namakkiruppatu pōṉṟē moḻiyaṟṟa māntaṉukkum iruntaṉa. ivvuṇmaiyai iṉṟum ūmaiyariṭattuk kāṇka. moḻiyamainta piṉpum, māntaṉ karuttiṟ perumpakuti uruva lippē yeṉpatai ōrntuṇarka.
iv iyaṟkai moḻi (tōrā. ki.mu. 1,00,000-50,000)
eḻuttup palukkamum (uccarippum) coṟporuttum iṉṟip perumpālum iyalpākap pēcappaṭum olittokuti, iyaṟkai moḻiyām (Natural Language). itu muḻaittalmoḻi (Inarticulate Speech) eṉappaṭum, itaṉ olikaḷ eḻuvakaiya:
(1) uṇarcci yolikaḷ (Emotional Sounds)
iṉpa tuṉpa uṇarcciyai veḷiyiṭum olikaḷ uṇarcci yolikaḷ.
e-ṭu: ā, ī, ū, ō, ai
(2) viḷiyolikaḷ (Vocative Sounds)
piṟarai viḷikkum olikaḷ viḷiyolikaḷ. viḷittal -kūppiṭutal.
e-ṭu: ē, ēy, ō, ēlā, ellā.
(3) oppolikaḷ (Imitative Sounds)
irutiṇaip poruḷkaḷum ceyyum olikaḷai ottolippavai oppolikaḷ.
e-ṭu: kū (kūvu), kā kā-(kākam), kākkā (kākkai), im (imiḻ), ur (uraṟu), ūḷ (ūḷai), kur (kuraṅku), mā (māṭu), cīttu (cīṟu), ō (ōcai), kar (karai), caracara (cārai), cal- (calaṅkai-cataṅkai), kīr-kīri.
kalakala, kiṇ, kaṇīr, kiriccu, caḷ, caḷār, calacala, tiṇ, maṭakku, viccu, vīr eṉpaṉavum, ivai pōṉṟa piṟavum, oppolikaḷē. ivai iṉṟu olikkuṟippuc coṟkaḷ eṉappaṭum. ōkkaḷi-ōkkāḷam, umaṭṭu-uvaṭṭu, kuṟaṭṭai, cappu, tuppu, tummu, vikku, mukku, ici, ciri mutaliyaṉa oppoli yaṭippaṭaiyiṟ piṟkālattup piṟanta coṟkaḷām.
iṉi, vāyoliyiṉṟi vāyvaḻiyum mūkkuvaḻiyum cevippula ṉākum kāṟṟumaṭṭum veḷivarum ceyalkaṭkum, oppoli muṟaiyil coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: ā-āvi, ū-ūtu, mūcu-mūccu.
koṭṭāvi viṭumpōtu āveṉṟum, nerupperik kavum viḷakkaṇaikkavum cūṭāṉa poruḷai āṟṟavum nōvu tīrkkavum ūtumpōtu ūveṉṟum olittaṟkēṟpa, vāyiṉiṉṟu kāṟṟu veḷi varutalum; urakka mūccu viṭumpōtum iḷaikkum pōtum, mūcu eṉṟu kēṭkumāṟu mūkkiṉiṉṟu kāṟṟu veḷivarutalum; kāṇka.
	āvittal = koṭṭāvi viṭutal, perumūccu viṭutal, vāy viṭutal.
	āvi = koṭṭāvi, uyirppu, neṭṭuyirppu,uyir, ātaṉ (āṉmā),  nīrāvi, pukai, naṟumaṇam, uyireḻuttu.
	ūtutal = ūtuvatu pōl turuttiyāl kāṟṟeḻup putal, ūtiya turuttiyum paiyum pōl vīṅkutal, paruttal, mikutal, vaṇṭu kuḻalūtu vatu pōl icaittukkoṇṭu tēṉai nukartal, ūtukuḻalpōl vaṇṭu marattait tuḷaittal.
	ūtu-ūti. ūtu-ūtili = makuṭi, viḷaiyāṭṭūti.
	ūtu-ūtiyam (ilāpam). celaviṉum mikuntatu ūtiyam.
	ūtu-ūttu-ūttam,
	ūtu-ūtāri = tukaḷai ūtit tuṭaittal pōl celvattai vīṇ celaviṭṭup pōkkupavaṉ.
	o - nō:	blow the expense = to spend recklessly.
		   	blow = to squander, spend (sum) recklessly.
	ūtu-ōtu.ōtutal = kātiṟkuḷ mellac collutal.

(4) kuṟippolikaḷ (Symbolic Sounds)
vaḻakkappaṭi cila karuttukaḷaip piṟarkkut teriyum vakaiyil kuṟikkum olikaḷ kuṟippolikaḷām.
eḻutamuṭivaṉavum eḻutamuṭiyātaṉavumāka, kuṟippoli kaḷ iruvakaiya. ūm(ūṅkoṭṭutal), cī, pū eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai eḻutamuṭivaṉa. moccaṭṭamum (moccak koṭṭutal - smacking), muṟkum (clucking), vīḷaiyum (cīḻkkai- whistling) pōṉṟavai eḻuta muṭiyātaṉa.
tō tō (tuvā tuvā) eṉṟu nāyaiyum, pēpē (pō pō) eṉṟu kōḻiyaiyum, vēcu vēcu eṉṟu pūṉaiyaiyum, pāy pāy eṉṟu āṭṭaiyum viḷikkum olikaḷ viḷiyoli kaḷāyiṉum vaḻakkampaṟṟik kuṟippāl uṇarappaṭuvatāṟ kuṟippolikaḷām.
(5) vāycceykai yolikaḷ (Gesticulatory Sounds)
māntaṉ iyaṟkaiyākavum ceyaṟkaiyākavum taṉ vāyiṉāṟ ceyyum cila ceykaikaḷum caikaikaḷum, ovvōr oliyaip piṟappittaṟkēṟṟa vāyvaṭivai amaittu, av volikaḷiṉ vāyilāy ac coṟkaṭku mūlamāṉa coṟ kaḷaip piṟappit      tirukkiṉṟaṉa. ac coṟkaṭku mūlamāṉa av volikaḷ vāyc ceykai yolikaḷām.
e-ṭu: ā-a-aṅkā. aṅkāttal = vāytiṟattal.
         av-kav-vav. av-avvu-kavvu-vavvu.
oṉṟaik kavvutalaiyotta vāyccaikai nilai, av eṉṉum oliyait tōṟṟuvittaṟ kēṟṟatātal kāṇka. mēlvāyp pal kīḻutaṭṭoṭu poruntuvatē kavvum nilaiyām. in nilai vakaramey yolippiṟkē ēṟkum.
piṟkālattil avvutal eṉṉum col maṉattiṉāṟ paṟṟu talukkum, kavvutal eṉṉum col vāyiṉāṟ paṟṟutalukkum, vavvutal eṉṉum col kaiyiṉāṟ paṟṟutalukkum varaiyaṟukkap peṟṟaṉa. āyiṉum, iṉṟum, avvutal ulaka vaḻakkil vāyiṉāṟ paṟṟutalai uṇarttum.

icittal eṉpatu olipaṭac cirittalaiyum, iḷittal eṉpatu oliyiṉṟic cirittalaiyum kuṟikkum eṉa vēṟu pāṭaṟika.

(6) kuḻavivaḷarp polikaḷ (Nursery Sounds)
kuḻavivaḷarppil kuḻavikaḷ iyalpāka olikkum olikaḷi ṉiṉṟu tāymār cila coṟkaḷai amaittuk koḷvatuṇṭu. attakaiya oli kaḷ kuḻavivaḷarppolikaḷ. avai mikac cilavē.
e-ṭu: iṅku - iṅkā = pāl.
kuḻavip paruvattiṉum periyatu kuḻantaip paruvam. ap paruvattil, cōṟu eṉpataic cō, cōy, cōci eṉṟu kuḻan taikaḷ koccaiyāyc colpavai kuḻavi vaḷarp polikaḷākā. ṭum ṭum (tom tom), Õ¥Õ(pipe) eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavai kuḻantaikaḷ collum oppolic coṟkaḷ.
kuḻavikaḷ mama, papa eṉṟu iyalpāka aṭukki olippatiṉiṉṟu āṅkila naṟṟāyarum cevilit tāyarum mammā (mamma), pappā (papa) eṉṉum muṟaippeyarkaḷai amaittukkoṇṭatāka moḻi nūlāciriyar cilar kūṟuvar. āyiṉ, āṅkilam tōṉṟu muṉpē, vaṭacēmiyak kiḷaiyaic cērnta aramiya (Aramaic) moḻiyil ammā, appā eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkiyiruk kiṉṟaṉa. cumēriya nākarikam tamiḻa nākarikattiṉ tiripātalālum, kumarināṭṭu makkaḷē āppirikka vaḻiyāka airōppā ceṉṟu paravi yiruppatākat terivatālum, ammā appā eṉṉuñ tamiḻccoṟkaḷē āṅkilattil mammā pappā eṉṟu tirintuḷḷaṉa veṉṟu koḷvatē poruttamām. akaramutal pakaramutalākat tamiḻilum tiraviṭattilum kūṭat tiritaluṇṭu.
e-ṭu :	añcavaṉ-pañcavaṉ = aiñciṟṟaracaraik koṇṭu   	āṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ,
	appaḷam-pappaṭam (ma.)
tamiḻa māntar kuṟiñci nākarika nilaiyilēyē māṭṭaip pāliṟkāka vaḷarkkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatākat terivatāl, tāyai viṭṭu nīṅkiya kaṉṟukkuṭṭiyiṉ kataṟaliṉiṉṟu, ammā eṉṉuñ collait tamiḻar aṉṉaiyaik kuṟikka amaittukkoṇṭaṉar eṉṟu koḷvatu mikap porutta mām. piṉpu, atu appā eṉṟu valittut tantaiyaik kuṟittirukkiṉṟatu. melliṉa meyccol meṉmai mikka tāyaiyum, valliṉa meyccol vaṉmai mikka tantaiyaiyum, kuṟippatu iyaṟkaiyē.
(7) cuṭṭolikaḷ (Deictic Sounds)
cuṭṭikkāṭṭum olikaḷ cuṭṭolikaḷ.
cēymaiyaic cuṭṭutaṟkēṟpa vāyai virivāyt tiṟan tolikkum ākāramum, cēymaikkup piṟpaṭṭa aṇmaiyaic cuṭṭutaṟkēṟpa vāyaip piṉṉukkiḻut tolikkum īkāramum, cēymaikkum aṇmaikkum iṭaippaṭṭa muṉmaiyaic cuṭṭutaṟ kēṟpa itaḻkaḷai muṟpaṭak kuvittolikkum ūkāramum, muṟaiyē cēymaiyaṇmai muṉmaic cuṭṭolikaḷāyiṉa. ivaiyē mutaṉ mutal tamiḻil tōṉṟiya uyirkaḷ. ivai uṇmai yil vāyccaikai yolikaḷākum. āyiṉum, ciṟappu nōkki vēṟu pirikkappeṟṟaṉa.
mutaṟkaṇ mūviṭaccuṭṭu kaiccaikaiyiṉāl maṭṭum nikaḻntatu. piṉpu, kaiccaikaiyōṭu kūṭiya vāyccaikaiyi ṉāl nikaḻntatu, ataṉpiṉ, vāyccaikaiyiṉāl maṭṭum nikaḻntu varukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, av vāyccaikait taṉmai iṉṟu maṟain tuḷḷatu, muccuṭṭolikaḷum vāyccaikai yolikaḷāyiruppa taṉālēyē, avai tamiḻil ek karaṇiyattaiyiṭṭum piṟamoḻi kaḷiṟpōl iṭamāṟic cuṭṭuvatillai. itaṉāl, tamiḻoṭu ciṟitum peritum toṭar puḷḷa piṟa moḻic cuṭṭuc coṟkaṭ kellām, tamiḻc coṟkaḷē mūlameṉpatait teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷḷalām.
e-ṭu:
iṭam māṟātaṉa:
	camaṟkirutam  		tamiḻ	āṅkilam		tamiḻ
	tatra	=	aṅku	that	=	atu
	ika	=	iṅku	this	=	itu

2. tamiḻt tōṟṟam (ceyaṟkai moḻi)
(tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000)
mūvakaic cuṭṭolikaḷiṉiṉṟuñ coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyatē tamiḻt tōṟṟamām. itu ceyaṟkai moḻi (Artificial Language) allatu iḻaittal moḻi (Articulate Speech) eṉappaṭum.
muntiyal māntarmoḻi yellām icaippāṭṭākavē tōṉṟiṉa eṉpatu ecuppercaṉ karuttu. atu tavaṟām. uṇarcci viñciya kātal, iṉpam, veṟṟi, makiḻcci, tuṉpam mutaliya maṉa nilaikaḷilēyē muntiyal māntar-avaruḷḷum icaivuṇarcciyum, moḻiyāṟṟalum mikkavarē-icaimoḻi vāyilāyt tam karuttai veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. ikkālattum, iṉpa tuṉpavuṇarcci viñciya pōtu, tattam aṟivunilaik kēṟpa icaippāṭṭākavum ceyyuḷākavum tam karuttaic cilar veḷiyiṭutal kāṇka. mēlum, poruḷum oḻuṅku maṟṟa orucila icaiyolit tokuti kaḷiṉiṉṟu vevvēṟu poruḷum ilakkaṇa voḻuṅkumuḷḷa peyar viṉaiyiṭaic coṟkaḷum, mūviṭap peyarkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ tiripāṉa irutiṇai aimpāl mūviṭa īṟukaḷum, vēṟṟumai urupukaḷum kāla viṭainilaikaḷum, piṟavum, amaintaṉa veṉṟal, uttikkup poruntāta kūṟṟām.
urai, ceyyuḷ (allatu pāṭṭu) eṉṉum iruvakai moḻi naṭaiyuḷ, mutalil tōṉṟiyatu muṉṉatē. atuvum taṉittaṉi collākavē tōṉṟiṟṟu. ākavē, collē moḻiyalakām (Unit of speech). uḷḷattil tōṉṟum karuttu, atait terivikkum coṟṟoṭarkku muṟṟum ottataṉṟu. amaiya vēṟupāṭṭiṟ kēṟpa, oru collē pala coṟṟoṭarkkuriya pala karuttait terivikkalām. oru kuḻantai allatu nōyāḷi taṇṇīr eṉṟāl, atu,
(1)	eṉakkut taṇṇīrvēṇṭum;
(2)	atō pār! taṇṇīr;
(3)	veḷḷam vīṭṭiṟkuḷ vantuviṭṭatu;
(4)	taṇṇīr koṇṭuvā;
(5)	intap pāl taṇṇīr kalantatu;
(6)	ic col taṇṇīr;
(7)	itaṉ poruḷ taṇṇīr;
(8)	iv viṭukatai viṭai taṇṇīr;
(9)	ip paṭattiluḷḷatu taṇṇīr;
(10)	(Cold) water eṉṉum āṅkilac coṟku nēr teṉ col `taṇṇīr
eṉṟu pala karuttait terivippatuṭaṉ, nī kuṭippatu eṉṉa? eṉpatupōṉṟa viṉāviṟku viṭaiyākavumiruttal kāṇka. naṉṟāyp pēcat teriyāta kuḻantaiyum, pēccu valimaiyaṟṟa nōyāḷiyum, tāymoḻiyoṉṟē terinta ayal nāṭṭāṉum, perumpālum taṉic coṟkaḷālēyē tam karuttait terivittal kāṇka. in nilaiyilēyē moḻi vaḷarcciyuṟāta muntiyal māntaṉum iruntāṉ.
moḻi eḷiya nilaiyil tōṉṟip paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarnta māntaṉ amaippēyaṉṟi, iṟaivaṉāṟ paṭaikkappeṟṟu iyaṟkaiyāka aṟiyappaṭṭataṉṟu. moḻi iyaṟkaiyāṉatāyiṉ ellā makkaḷum kallāmalē orē moḻi pēcutalvēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamillai. makkaṭ kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ nākarika nilaikkut takkavāṟu, moḻikaḷ vevvēṟu vakaippaṭṭum nilaippaṭṭum amaippuk koṇṭum uḷḷaṉa. cila anākarika māntar moḻikaḷ paṟavai vilaṅkolikaḷiṉum caṟṟē uyarntavaiyākum. ettuṇai uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyāyiṉum, talaiciṟanta aṟiviyal vaḷarcci peṟṟa makkaḷāyiṉum, ovvoru collāy mella  mellak kaṟṟālaṉṟi oruvarum tam tāymoḻiyaip pēcaviyalātu.
moḻittuṇaiyiṉṟiyum māntarkkuk karuttu nikaḻum. ūmaiyar nilai itaṟkut talaiciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭām. aḵṟiṇaiyuyirikaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyum itaṟku oruvakaic cāṉ ṟākum. moḻiyiṉṟik karuttu nikaḻāteṉpatu, ārāycciyil lār kūṟṟē. viṉaikkut tuṇaikkoḷvatu pōṉṟatē. uṇarcci, vēṭkai, niṉaippu, cūḻvu, tīrmāṉam, iṉpam (uvakai), tuṉpam (aḻukai) mutaliya eṇ allatu toṇ (oṉpāṉ) cuvainilai, aiyam (cantēkam), teḷivu ākiyavaiyē maṉattoḻilkaḷ. ivaṟṟiṟku moḻittuṇai tēvaiyillai. kāṭcipporuḷka ḷellām maṉattilum tōṉṟumātalāl, niṉaippum cūḻvum cUtÈ¥ghfnt(imagination) nikaḻum.
moḻi vaḷarcciyaṭainta piṟkālattilum, coṟṟoṭar kaḷiluḷḷa ellā ilakkaṇak kūṟukaṭkum otta pakutikaḷ uḷḷak karuttilillai. pāṟkuṭam eṉpataip pālaiyuṭaiya kuṭam eṉa virippar ilakkaṇiyar. av virippiluḷḷa aikāravēṟṟumai yurupum uṭaiyaveṉṉum kuṟippup peyareccamum, uḷḷak karuttilillai. atiluḷḷavai yellām pālum ataik koṇṭa kuṭamumē. iṅṅaṉamē, viṉaimuṟṟiluḷḷa aṟuvakai yuṟuppukaḷuḷ, mutaṉilai yiṭainilai yiṟutinilaikkuriya karuttu kaḷē uḷḷattiluṇṭu. avaiyum moḻiyil oruṅkē tōṉṟa villai. mutalil mutaṉilaiyē irutiṇai aimpāl mūviṭaṅ kaṭkum uriya ellā viṉaivaṭiviṟkum potuvāyiruntatu; piṉpu pālīṟum kāla viṭai nilaiyum eccamuṟṟu vēṟupāṭum muṟaiyē tōṉṟiṉa. iv vuṇmaiyellām tamiḻpōṉṟa iyaṉ moḻi vāyilākavē aṟiyamuṭiyum; camaṟkirutam pōṉṟa tirimoḻiyai yum ceyaṟkaimoḻiyaiyum aṭippaṭaiyāy vaittārāyiṉ, airōppiyarāyiṉum amerikka rāyiṉum kāriruḷiṟ kāṭṭuvaḻic celvārpōl oṉṟuṅ kaṇṭaṟiyār.
cuṭṭaṭiccollākkam
muccuṭṭukaḷuḷ, cēymaiccuṭṭiṉiṉṟu cēymaik karuttut tavira vēṟoṉṟum piṟattaṟ kiṭamillai; aṇmaiccuṭṭiṉiṉṟu, aṇmai, piṉmai, iḻikai mutaliya orucila karuttukaḷē piṟak kiṉṟaṉa; āyiṉ, muṉmaic cuṭṭiṉiṉṟē, tōṉṟal (muṉvarutal), muṉmai, muṟcellal, neruṅkal, poruntal, vaḷaital, tuḷaittal, turuval ākiya eṇperuṅ karuttukaḷum, ivaṟṟiṟku iṭaippaṭṭaṉavum ivaṟṟiṟ kiḷaittaṉavumāṉa ettuṇaiyō nuṇkaruttu kaḷum piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟai yellām eṉ `mutaṟṟāy moḻi'yuṭ kaṇṭukoḷka. iṅku eṇperuṅ karuttukaḷē iṉṟiyamaiyāta aḷavu viḷakkappeṟum.
tōṉṟal eṉpatu, tāyiṉiṉṟu kuḻaviyum marattiṉiṉṟu tuḷirum tōṉṟuvatupōṉṟa iyaṟkait tōṉṟalum; vīṭṭi ṉiṉṟu māntaṉum vaḷaiyiṉiṉṟu eliyum tōṉṟuvatu pōṉṟa ceyaṟkait tōṉṟalum, āka iruvakaippaṭum. tōṉṟutal muṉ varutalātaliṉ, tōṉṟaṟkaruttilēyē taṟkiḻamai piṟitiṉ kiḻamai ākiya iruvakai muṉmaik karuttum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. muṉkiḷai yum muṉkaiyum pōṉṟavai taṟkiḻamai muṉmai; muṉ poṟaiyum (muṉpāramum) muṉtūtaṉum pōṉṟavai piṟitiṉkiḻamai muṉmai.
tōṉṟiyapiṉ, uṭaṉēyō kālañceṉṟō, ōr iṭam nōkkic celavu allatu palaticaiyum paṭarcci ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. iyaṟkaiyāṉa celavellām muṉṉōkkiyatē yātaliṉ, tōṉ ṟalum celavum, muṟaiyē, muṉvarukaiyum muṟcelavu mēyākum. iv viru poruṭkum aṭippaṭaiyāṉa muṉmaik karuttai ūkārac cuṭṭē uṇarttukiṉṟatu. ūkārattai allatu ukarattai olikkumpōtu, itaḻ kuvintu muṉṉiṭattaic cuṭṭuvatai allatu nōkkuvataik kāṇka. cēmparār āṅkilac coṟpiṟappiyal akara KjÈÆš(Chamber’s Etymological Dictionary), move eṉṉum coṟku moḻipporuṭkaraṇiyam itaḻ muṉṉōkkik kuvin tolittal eṉṟu kuṟittiruttilaik kūrntu nōkkuka.
muṟcellaccella, cēravēṇṭiya iṭattiṟku nerukkam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. cērntapiṉ poruntal nērkiṉṟatu. iṭaivaḻiyiṟ cuvarum kallum malaiyumpōlat taṭai ēṟpaṭiṉ, vaḷaiya allatu pakkamākat tirumpa nērkiṉṟatu. taṭuttaporuḷaiyum iṭattaiyum tuḷaikka muṭiyumāyiṉ, eli cuvaraiyum māntaṉ malaiyaiyum tuḷaittal nērkiṉṟatu.. tuḷaittu maṟupuṟaṅ kāṇiṉ atuvē turuval. ataṉpiṉ tōṉṟal mutaliya paḻaiya nilaimaikaḷē mīṇṭum nikaḻum. maṇṇil vēr iṟaṅkutalum, marattil āṇi patitalum, pottakattiṟ puḻuvarittalum pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷāyiṉ, vaḷaitaliṉṟiyē tuḷait talum turuvalum nikaḻum.
irutiṇai yuyirikaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyaiyum nōkkiṉāl, avaṟṟiṉ ceyalkaḷellām iv veṇvakaik karuttu vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷēyē amaikiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟaik kūrntu kavaṉitta paṇṭait tamiḻar, tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattiṉāl, itupō tuḷḷa tamiḻc coṟṟokutiyuḷ ēṟattāḻa mukkāṟpaṅkai ūkārac cuṭṭaṭiyākavē tōṟṟuvittiruk kiṉṟaṉar.

uvaṉ	=	muṉniṟpavaṉ
utu	=	muṉniṟpatu
ukaraccuṭṭuccoṟkaḷellām paṭarkkaipōṟ payaṉ paṭuttappaṭiṉum, pēcuvāṉukku muṉṉiṭattaiyum atiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷaiyumē cuṭṭum. ātalāl ukaraccuṭṭu eṉṟum muṉmaic cuṭṭēyaṉṟi, cēymaikkum aṇmaikkum iṭaip paṭṭa iṭaimaic cuṭṭaṉṟu. itu nīṇṭakālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyiṟṟu. āyiṉ, yāḻppāṇattil ōraḷavu vaḻakkiluḷḷatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ukarac cuṭṭaṭiccoṟkaḷ
ukaraccuṭṭuvēr taṉittum ka, ca, ta, na, pa, ma eṉṉum aṟu moḻimuta leḻuttukaḷōṭu kūṭiyum varum. ñavayameykaḷ ukarattōṭukūṭi moḻimutal varāmaiyāl, avai piṟavuyir kaḷōṭu kūṭi moḻimutal varum coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa, ukarac cuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷiṉ tiripākavē yirukkum.  uyirukaram mutalāka varuvatu mutalaṭi; uyirmey yukaram mutalāka varuvatu vaḻiyaṭi. collākkattil, poruḷmayakkattai nīkkutaṟku, uyirum meyyum palvē ṟeḻuttāyt tirikiṉṟaṉa. at tiripukaḷellām caṟṟup piṉṉark kūṟappaṭum. iṅku, uyirttiripil u-i, u-o eṉpavaṟṟaiyum, meyttiripil l-r, l-ḷ, ḻa-ka eṉpavaṟṟaiyum uḷḷattil iruttutalvēṇṭum.
1. tōṉṟutal (muṉvarutal)
	u-ul-ur-uru.
uru: 	uruttal = tōṉṟutal. uru-(aru)-arumpu,
	arumputal = tōṉṟutal. aruppam = tōṉṟum mīcai.
	uru = karu. uru = tōṟṟam, vaṭivam, niṟam. uru - uruvu - 	uruvam. uruvu - urupu = vēṟṟumai vaṭivam.
kuru:	kuruttal = tōṉṟutal. kuru - kuruttu = tōṉṟum 		iḷavōlai. 
	kuruttu-kuruntu = veṇkuruttu, kuḻantai. kuruntu-karuntu =  marakkaṉṟu (kōṭai.). kuru-kurumpai = teṉṉai paṉaiyiṉ iḷaṅkāy. kuru-kuruku = kuruttu, kuṭṭi, 
	kuru-karu=1. cūl. karuccitait tōrkkum	(puṟam. 34)
	2. kuḻantai. cērartaṅ karuvai 	(pārata.nirai.116)
	3. kuṭṭi. kācaṟaik karuvum	(cilap. 25:52)
	4. piṟappu. karuvait tuṭaippa	(pirapu. kokki. 15)
	5. muṭṭaikkaru. puṟavukkaru vaṉṉa	(puṟam. 34)
	6. nilattil tōṉṟum poruḷ. karuveṉa moḻipa           (tol. 964)                        
curu
JU:	turu-turuttu eṉpatu iṉṟu muṉtaḷḷutalaik kuṟikkum.
	nuru = piñcu. nuru-noru = iḷaṅkatir, piñcu.
puru: 	puru = kuḻantai. puru-(piru)-piruku = muṟṟāp paṉaṅkiḻaṅku.
	piruku - pirukku.
	ciṟu piñcukaḷaip piñcum pirukkum eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku.
muru:	muru-muruku = iḷamai, iḷamaiyāṉa murukaṉ. muruku - murukaṉ. muru-muruntu = kuruttu, taḷir.
	muru-muṟi=taḷir. muṟital = tuḷirttal. muṟinta kōlamukiḻ (cīvaka. 2358)
	muru - maru - maruvu. maruvutal = tōṉṟutal.
uru eṉṉum aṭikku muntiyatu ul eṉpatākum. lakaram  ḷakaramākat tirivatu pōṉṟē ḷakaram ḻakaramākat tiriyum.
	ul-ullari = taḷir, ulavai=taḻai. īyulavaiyiṉōppi (iraku. tēṉu. 35).
	kul - kuḷ - kuḷaku = taḷir, taḻai. kuḷ - kuṭṭi. kuḷ - kuḻa - kuḻai =    taḷir. kuḻa - koḻuntu. kuḻa - kuḻavu - kuḻaku - kuḻakaṉ. kuḻa - kuḻantai. kuḻavu - kuḻavi.
	cul-cūl = karu, muṭṭai. cul-cil-ciṉṉa-ciṉai. ciṉaittal = tōṉṟutal, arumputal, karukkoḷḷu tal, taḻaittal. ciṉai=  moṭṭu, marakkiḷai, cūl, muṭṭai.
	tul-tuḷ-tuḷir-taḷir. tuḷ-taḷ-taḻu-taḻukku, taḻukkutal= taḻaittal. taḻu-taḻai = kuḻai.
	nul - (nuḷ) - nuḻu - nuḻuval = iḷampākku. nuḻu - nuḻāy = iḷam pākku, nul-(nuṉ)-nuṉai-naṉai = arumpu. naṉaital = arumputal, tōṉṟutal.
	pul-(pal)-pallavam = (va.) = taḷir, pul-(puḷ)-piḷ-piḷḷai . piḷ = piḷḷaimaiyaḻaku, piḷ - pīḷ = iḷaṅkatir, iḷamai, karu. puḷ-pūḷ-pūṭṭai = iḷaṅkatir. pūṭṭai - pīṭṭai.
	mul-muḷ-muḷai. muḷaittal = tōṉṟutal. muḷaiyaṉ = ciṟuvaṉ-muḷ-muṭṭu-moṭṭu. muḷ-maḷ-maḷḷaṉ =   iḷaiñaṉ.  maḷ - maḻa - maḻavu - maḻavaṉ = iḷaiñaṉ. 
maḻavum  kuḻavum iḷamaip poruḷa 	(tol.uri.14)
	maḻa - maḻalai - matalai = iḷamai, piḷḷai.   
	ḻakaram cilaviṭattuk kakaramākat tiriyum.
	e-ṭu: toḻuti - tokuti, muḻai-mukai.
	ivvakaiyil uḻu eṉṉum aṭi uku eṉṟākum.
uku -  (ukai) - akai. akaital = taḷirttal. 		      
koykuḻai akaikāñci  	(kalit. 74)
	kuku - kukai - kukaiccu (puṟṟu)
	cuku - cukai (kottal, kiḻittal) - cukam (kiḷi)
	tuku - (teku) - tikai - (muṭivu, ellai)
	nuku - nukumpu = paṉaṅkuruttu. nuku - nuṅku = paṉañcuḷai, 		iḷampaṉaṅkoṭṭai.
	puku - 	pokil = arumpu. pokil - pōkil = arumpu. 			pōku - pōkku = marakkaṉṟu (piṅ.)
	puku - 	pō-pōttu = iḷaṅkiḷai.
		pō - pōntu (piṅ.) = iḷampaṉai. pōntu-pōntai 		= (tivā.) iḷampaṉai.
	muku - mukiḻ arumpu. mukiḻtal = arumputal. mukiḻttal = 		tōṉṟutal, arumputal, īṉutal. muku-mukai = arumpu. 		muku- mokku-mokkuḷ = arumpu.
	mukiḻ - mukiḻam = pērarumpu. mukiḻ - mukiḻi. mukiḻittal = 		arumpu pōṟ kuvital.
mukiḻam eṉṉum col, arumpu eṉṉum poruḷil, mukula, mukura, makula, makura eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
kumarikkaṇṭa muḻukkiṉālum, mutaliru kaḻaka vilakkiya muḻutum kaṭaikkaḻaka vilakkiyap perumpakutiyum aḻintu pōṉamaiyālum nīṇṭakālamākat tamiḻar tamiḻaip pēṇāmai yālum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉcoṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ataṉālēyē, pala mutalaṭic coṟkaṭkum vaḻiyaṭic coṟkaṭkum eṭuttukkāṭṭa iyalavillai yeṉṟaṟika. iṟantupaṭṭa coṟka ḷellām piṟaikkōṭṭuḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷa. oru poṟiyaṟiñaṉ oru poṟiyilillāta uṟuppaik kaṇṭukoḷvatu pōṉṟē, oru moḻiyaṟiñaṉum am moḻiyil iṟantupaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟai avaṟṟoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa piṟacoṟkaḷiṉ tuṇai koṇṭu aṟiyaviyalum eṉka.
2. muṉmai
ūṅku = muṉpu
ummai = muṟpiṟappu. ummaiviṉai.... oḻiyātu   (maṇimē. 26:32)
kuṉai = kūriya muṉpakkam allatu mēṟpakkam.  nuṉi - muṉai.
nuṉi - nuti = muṉ. naṭantāḷ nuti   	 (cīvaka. 1933, urai) 
nuti - tutu = muṉ, nuṉi. tutikkai = yāṉaiyiṉ muṉṉuḷḷa allatu kūriya nuṉiyuḷḷa kai.
muṉ -muṉi. muṉ-muṉai=muṉpakkam. muku-mukam = muṉpakkam. mukam-mukar-mukarai. mukam- mukaṉ = mukaṉai-mōṉai.
mukam eṉṉum col muṉmaikkaruttaiyum tōṉṟaṟ karuttaiyum iṇaittukkāṭṭutal kāṇka. ic col vaṭamoḻiyil aramāya eṉṟu tiriyum.
mukañceytal	1.	muṉṉātal. tōṟṟiṉāṉ 		 		mukañcey	kōlam       	 (cīvaka.675)                  
		2.	tōṉṟutal. mukañcey kārikai  			(peruṅ. uñcaik.35:49)
3. muṉcellutal (cellutal)
ukaital = cellutal, ukaittal = celuttutal.
ukai-akai. akaittal = celuttutal. utaittal = celuttutal.
iccilai yutaittakōṟ kilakkam  	(kamparā. kārmuka.9)
untutal = celuttutal.
uytal = cellutal, tappic cellutal. uyttal =   celuttutal 
uy-oy. oytal=celuttutal,
uy-iy-iyal. iyalutal=cellutal, naṭattal.
iy -iya. iyattal = cellutal, kaṭattal.
iya - iyavu = celavu.
iya - iyaṅku. iyaṅkutal = acaital, cellutal.
iya-ē-ēku. o.nō: viyar- vēr.
(cul)-cal-cel.
ca-ce. o.nō: cattāṉ-cettāṉ.
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻil, cal eṉṉum aṭiyum cellutal viṉaiyaik kuṟittiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉṟu atu ap poruḷil, vaṭatiraviṭa vaḻippaṭṭa intiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
turattal = celuttutal. turattutal = viraivākac celuttutal.
muṉmaikkum muṟcelaviṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa karuttu muṉ taḷḷutal.
muṉtaḷḷutal
uttutal = muṉ taḷḷutal, kaḻittal.
untutal = muṉ taḷḷutal. untal - uñcal - ūñcal- ūcal.
uṉṉutal = muṉ taḷḷutal.
(tuḷ)-taḷ. o.nō: tuḷir-taḷir.
tuntu - tunti = muṉ taḷḷiya vayiṟu. tunti - tonti.
turuttutal = tonti vayiṟupōl muṉ taḷḷutal.
turutti = kāṟṟai muṉ taḷḷum kollulait tōṟkaruvi  allatu icaikkaruvi. turutti - tutti - titti = icaikkaruvi.
tūṇṭutal = viḷakkut tiriyai muṉ taḷḷutal. tūṇṭu = tīṇṭu
nuntutal = tūṇṭutal. nuntā viḷakku=tūṇṭā viḷakku. nuntu-nontu.
nūkkutal = muṉ taḷḷutal, taḷḷutal.
uḷḷattai muṉ taḷḷutal (ūkkutal)
uy-uyal-uyaṟṟu-uñaṟṟu.
uñaṟṟutal = muyaṟcikkut tūṇṭutal, varunti yuḻaittal.
uḷḷutal = uḷḷattai ūkkutal. uḷḷam=ūkkam.
ūkkutal = uḷḷattai muṟceluttutal. ūṅku-ūkku.
tūṇṭutal = ūkkutal, ēvutal.
nūkkutal = tūṇṭutal.
muyalutal = taṉ uḷḷattai ūkkutal.
Push, shoot, usher, urge, duct, duke, thrust mutaliya āṅkilac coṟkaḷiṉ vērpporuḷaiyum ukara vaṭivaiyum, ivaṟṟuṭaṉ oppu nōkka.
4. neruṅkutal, ceṟital, kūṭutal
pala poruḷ neruṅkutal ceṟitalām.
(uḷ)-aḷ. uḷḷutal = neruṅkutal, ceṟital. aḷ-aṇ. aṇṇutal = neruṅkutal.
(kuḷ)-(kuṭṭu)-kiṭṭu. kiṭṭutal = neruṅkutal.
(cuḷ)-(ceḷ)-ceṟu. ceṟuttal = ceṟital.
ceṟu-ceṟi.
tul-tuṉ. tuṉṉutal = neruṅkutal. tuṉ - tuṉṟu. tuṉṟutal =  neruṅkutal. 
(nuḷ) - naḷ - naṇ. naḷḷutal = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal.        	           naṇṇutal = neruṅkutal.
(nuḷ) - (neḷ) - neru - neruṅku.
mul - muḷ - muru - maru - maruvu. maruvutal = kiṭṭutal.
mul- mal - mali - malital = neruṅkutal. niṟaital. mikutal.
(muḷ) - (maḷ) - maṇṭu. maṇṭutal = neruṅkutal, kūṭutal. 
maṇṭu - maṇṭakam = makkaḷ kūṭum kāraikkūṭam. maṇṭakam - maṇṭapam-(va.) maṇṭapa.
o-nō: vāṇikam-vāṇipam.
5. poruntutal, kūṭutal, ottal
ul-u
uttal=poruntutal. utti=viḷaiyāṭṭil iruvar  cērntu varum cērkkai, aṟiviṟkup poruttamāṉa ilakkaṇa neṟimuṟai.
ul-ol. ollutal=poruntutal. ol-oṉ.
oṉṉutal = poruntutal. oṉ-oṉṟu.
oṉṟutal = poruntutal. ol-(oḷ)-oṇ.
oṇṇutal = poruntutal. oḷ-oṭṭu.
ol-o. ottal = poruntutal.
kul-kulavu. kulavutal = kūṭutal.
kul-(kuḷ)-kaḷ. kaḷḷutal = ottal, kūṭutal.
kaḷḷa = pōla. kaḷ-kaḷam = kūṭṭam kūṭumiṭam. ērkkaḷam,  pōrkkaḷam, avaikkaḷam eṉpavaṟṟai nōkkuka.
(kuḷ)-kuḻu - kuḻuvu. kuḻu - kuḻumal = kūṭṭam.
kuḻu-kuḻumpu = tiraḷ. kuḻu - kuḻām = kumpal.
(kuḷ) - (kūḷ) - kūṇṭu - kūṭu - kūṭṭam.
(cuḷ) - ceḷ - ceru - cēr.
tul - tulliyam = oppu. tul - tulai = oppu.
tul - tuṉ. tuṉṉutal = poruntutal.
tul - tuṉṟu. tuṉṟutal = poruntutal.
(nuḷ) - naḷ - naṇ - naṇpu. naḷ - naṭpu.
pullutal = poruntutal, ottal, taḻuvutal, kūṭutal.
pul - pol - poru - poruntu - poruttu - pottu  - poṭṭu.
pol - pōl. pōlutal = ottal.
poru - poruvu = oppu.
pul - puḷ - (puṇ) - puṇar. (puṇ) - pūṇ - pūṭṭu.
pul - (pur) - purai. puraital = ottal.
mul - muḷ - muḷku. muḷkutal = taḻuvutal.
muḷ - muḻu - muḻuvu. muḻuvutal = muttutal, taḻuvutal.
mul - mal-maṉ. maṉṉutal = poruntutal.
maṉ - maṉṟu - maṉṟam. maṉ - maṉai.
6. vaḷaital
ul - ulā - ulavu - ulāvu. ulavutal = cuṟṟivarutal.
kul - kulā - kulavu. kulavutal = vaḷaital.
kul - kuḷ - koḷ - kōḷ - kōṇ - kōṭu - kōṭṭam.
cul - culā - culavu. culavutal = vaḷaital.
tul - (til) - tir - tirum. tirumutal = vaḷaital, cāytal, mīḷutal.
tirum - tirumpu.
(nuḷ) - neḷ - neḷi - neṟi.
neḷital = uṭal vaḷaital. neṟital = mayir curuḷtal.
pul - puru - puri. puru - puruvam = kaṇ mēlmayir vaḷaivu.
purital = vaḷaital. valampuri iṭampuri eṉṉum caṅkukaḷai nōkkuka.
(mul) - muru - muruku = vaḷainta kātaṇi. muru - muṟu - muṟṟu.
muṟṟutal = vaḷaital, cūḻtal, muṟṟukaiyiṭutal.
muru - muri. murital = vaḷaital.
ul - (ur) - uruḷ, to roll. uruḷ eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu kuruḷ (to curl), curuḷ (to coil) mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟantirut talaiyum, av vaṭiyai whirl, swirl, twirl eṉṟa āṅkilac coṟkaḷ oruvāṟot tiruttalaiyum nōkkuka. puruḷ eṉpatiṉiṉṟu puruṭai (ãUil, turning key of a lute string) eṉṉum collum, muruḷ eṉpatiṉiṉṟu KUF(knot in wood) eṉṉum collum, tirintuḷḷaṉa. uḻal (to revolve) eṉṉum aṭiyiṉiṉṟu FHš(to curl), RHš(to rotate) eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruttalum nōkkat takkatākum.
7. tuḷaittal
uḷ - uḷu = tuḷaikkum puḻu. uḷuttal = puḻu marattait tuḷaittal.
uḷu - uḻu. uḻutal = nilattaik kīṟutal.
kuḷ - kuḻi - kuḻai - kuṭai. kuṭaital = tuḷaittal.
kuḻi - koḻu - koḻutu - kōtu. kōtutal = kuṭaital.
koḻu = nilattait tuḷaikkum ērūci.
koḻu-kōḻi = kiḷaikkum paṟavai.
kuḷ- kuḷi. kuḷittal = uṭpukutal, muḻukutal, nīrāṭal.
cuḷ - cura - curai = uṭṭuḷai. cura - curaṅkam.
curaikkāy = kāyntapiṉ uṭṭuḷaiyamaiyuṅ kāy.
tul - tuṉ = elivaḷai. tuṉṉal = uḻutal.
tul - tuḷ - tuḷai. tuḷ - toḷ - toḷḷai - toḷai.
toḷ - tōḷ - tōṇṭu. toḷ - toṭu. toṭutal = tōṇṭutal. 
nuḷ - noḷ - noḷḷai = kuḻivu, kaṭkuḻivu.
noḷ-noḷḷal-ñeḷḷal  = paḷḷam.
nul-nuḻai - nūḻai = tuḷai, vāyil.
pul = uṭṭuḷai, uṭṭuḷaiyuḷḷa payirvakai allatu                               mūṅkil. pullāṅkuḻal = mūṅkiṟkuḻal.
puṟakkā ḻaṉavē pulleṉa moḻipa.      	(tol. 1585)
pul-puḷ-puḻu = tuḷaikkum ciṟṟuyir. puḻuttal = 	puḻuttuḷaittal.
puḷ-poḷ-poḷi-poṟi.
poḷḷal = tuḷai, tuḷaittal. poḷittal = tuḷaittal, veṭṭutal, kiḻittal.
poṟittal = kuḻitteḻututal.
muḷ-(muḻu) = muḻuku - mūḻku. muḻukutal = uṭpukutal, nīriṟ kuḷittal.
(muḻu)-muḻai = kukai. muḻaittal = tuḷaittal.
muḻai-mūḻai = akappai.
muḻuku-muḻuṅku-muṅku. muḻuṅku - viḻuṅku.
viḻuṅkutal = vāykkuḷ muḻukkutal.
muḷ - moḷ - moṇṭai-montai.
moṇṭai - maṇṭai. moḷḷutal = nīrpporuḷukkuḷ muḻukki yeṭuttal.
8. turuvutal
uḷ-uru - uruvu. uruvutal = kaikkuḷ oṉṟu turuvumāṟu iḻuttal.
kuḷ-(kuru)-kōr-kō. kōrvai-kōvai.
kōrttal = nūl ūcik kātilum pācit tuḷaiyilum turuvac ceytal.
cuḷ-curu. curuṅkai = nilattiṉkīḻt turuvic cellum vaḻi.
curu-curuvu-curuvam = uṇavuk kalattuḷ turuvum akappai.
tuḷ-turu-turuvu. turu-tūr - tūrtal = ūṭu cellutal, vācaluṭ pukutal. vīṭṭiṟkuḷ pukutalait tūrtal eṉpatu vaṭārk kāṭṭu vaḻakku.
tūr-tūri = nīrturuvic cellum mīṉpoṟi.
tūr-tōr - tōraṇam = teruvūṭu kaṭṭum aḻakut toṅkal.
tōr-tōraṇai=kōrvai.
puḷ-puḻu-puku-pukutu-pukuru-pūr. pūrtal = uṭpukutal.
pūr-pūrāṉ = maṇṇiṟkuḷ pukum naccuppūcci. pūr - pūṟu. pūṟutal = uruvattuḷaittal. mūkkuppūṟi-mūkkuppīṟi. pūṟu-  pīṟu.
mul-mūlam=vāyil (means).
3. tamiḻ vaḷarcci
i urainaṭai
kiḷaviyākkam
1. coṟṟirivu muṟaikaḷ
kiḷavi eṉpatu nālvakai ilakkaṇaviyaṟ colliṟkum potuvāṉa col. kiḷattal-collutal. kiḷappatu kiḷavi.
māntaṉ nākarikam vaḷara vaḷarak karuttukaḷ palkukiṉṟaṉa. aṭippaṭaik karuttukaḷiṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukaḷ kiḷaikkum pōtu, muṉṉavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ palvēṟu vakaiyil tiripaṭaikiṉṟaṉa. allākkāl palaporuḷoru brhšY«(polysemy) palacol byhUtoî«(homonymy) mikkup poruḷmayakku ēṟpaṭum. atait taṭukkac colvaṭivai māṟṟa vēṇṭiyuḷatu. cuḷ eṉṉum aṭi poruntaṟ poruḷiṟ ceḷ eṉṟu tiriyāviṭiṉ, cuṭutalaiyum uṟaittalaiyum kuṟikkuñ colloṭu atai mayakka nērum. ceḷ eṉṉum vaṭiviṉiṉṟē ceṇṭu, ceṇṭai, ceṭi, ceṇṇu, ceru, ceṟu, ceṟi, ceṟṟai, ceḻi, cēr mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa. nuḷ eṉpatu neruṅ kaṟporuḷil naḷ eṉṟu tiriyāviṭiṉ, kiḷḷutalaik kuṟikkum colloṭu atai mayakka nērum. mēlum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa karuttukaṭku vevvēṟu collum colvaṭivum vēṇṭiyiruppa tāl, vērccollum aṭiccollum palvēṟu tiripukoṇṭā loḻiya moḻi vaḷarccikkiṭamillai.

(6) palvakai īṟukaḷ
poruḷ māṟumpōtu collum māṟavēṇṭu meṉpatu, collākka aṭippaṭai neṟimuṟai. allāk kāl, poruḷ atuvō ituvō eṉa mayaṅka nērum. maṇ eṉṉuñ col maṇalaik kuṟit taṟku allīṟu peṟṟu maṇal eṉṟāṉamaiyum, kampu eṉṉuñ col, marakkōlaik kuṟiyātu kaṉiya (ulōka)k kōlaik kuṟittaṟku ikara vīṟu peṟṟuk kampiyeṉ ṟāṉamaiyum kāṇka. pakkattaiyum iṭuppai yum kuṟikkum maruṅku eṉṉum col, maruṅkul eṉa ullīṟu peṟṟē taṉippaṭa iṭuppaik kuṟittatu.
mutaṉ mutalākat tōṉṟiya īṟukaḷ, cuṭṭukaḷum cuṭṭaṭic coṟkaḷumākavē yiruntirukkiṉṟaṉa. cuṭṭeḻuttukaḷ, mutaṟ kālattil peyareccamāka maṭṭumaṉṟip peyarākavum irun tirukkiṉṟaṉa.
v-L: M=mJ(bg.), anta (pe.e.). ikkālattum atupōtu eṉṟu, cuṭṭuppeyar peyareccamāy vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
uyirolikaḷait taṉittolikkumpōtu, kuṟiliṉum neṭilē iḷañciṟārkkum muntiyal māntarkkum eḷitām. kuṟilai  viṭṭolikkumpōtu mūccuppai muyaṟci mikku vēṇṭi yiruttaliṉ, kīḻvakuppu māṇavar kuṟil neṭil vēṟupāṭiṉṟiyē uyireḻuttu kaḷai olittup pōtal kāṇka. itaṉāl uyireḻut tukaḷ mutalil neṭilākavē tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu uyttuṇarappaṭum.
a i uam mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu 		(31)
eṉṟu kūṟiya tolkāppiyaṉār, caṟṟup piṉpu,
neṭṭeḻut tēḻē ōrēḻut torumoḻi. 	(43)
kuṟṟeḻut taintum moḻiniṟai pilavē 	(44)
eṉṟu atai orumaruṅku maṟuttiruppatum. viṉā veḻuttukaḷaik kuṟikkumiṭattu,'
āē ōam mūṉṟum viṉāa 	(32)
eṉa ekarattai viṭṭiruppatum, tolkāppiyam neṭukalum āyiṭai'' eṉṉum toṭarē payiṉṟu varuvatum, āṇṭu īṇṭu eṉṉum coṟkaṭkuk kuṟukiya vaṭiviṉmaiyum, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭku iṉakkuṟil pintit tōṉṟiyuḷḷamaiyum, itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷākum.
cuṭṭīṟukaḷ
ā-uṇā, nilā, irā, viḷā, kaḷā, nuṇā, kaṭā, miḻā. 
ī-kurī (kurīi = kuruvi).
ū-koḷ-koḷum-koḷumū-koṇmū(mukil).
ciṟu paṟavai (kuruvi) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum kurī eṉṉum collaip piṟkālattār kurīi eṉa aḷapeṭaiyākki viṭṭaṉar.
koṇmū= kaṭalnīraik koḷḷum mukil. koḷḷutal= mukattal. koḷumutal = mukattal. itaṉ viḷakkattaic coṟ paṭai vaḷarcci eṉṉum talaippiṉkīḻk kāṇka.
āvīṟu muṟaiyē a, eṉat tiriyum.
e-ṭu: nilā-nilavu. irā-ira-iravu, kaḷā-kaḷavu. avu īṟu avam eṉṟum tiriyum.
e-ṭu: arā-ara-aravu-aravam. kaḷā-kaḷa-kaḷavu-kaḷavam.
mutaṟkālattil neṭilākavē yirunta muccuṭṭukaḷum piṟkālat til kuṟilākavum māṟiṉa.
e-ṭu:	ā-a, ātu-atu, āṅku-aṅku.
	ī-i, ītu-itu, īṅku-iṅku
	ū-u, (ūtu)-utu, ūṅku-uṅku.
eḻuttukaḷ taṉinilaiyiṉṟip puṇarnilaippaṭṭuc coṟ kaḷākumpōtu, neṭil vaṭiviṉuṅ kuṟilvaṭivē palukka (uccarikka) eḷitām.
īkāra ūkārac cuṭṭīṟukaḷ ikkālattu iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ kuṟil vaṭivukaḷē iṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
e-ṭu: a-uṇa, nila, viḷa, ira, (ce.va.).
         i-kaṇṇi, kiḷi, nari, puli, uṟumi, koṭi, veḷḷi.
	u-koḻu (kalappaik kāṟu), karu (cūl), urumu, varaku.
cila akaravīṟu avu eṉṟu tiriyum.
e-ṭu: kuḻa-kuḻavu, maḻa-maḻavu.
cuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaḷ
ellā meykaḷuḷḷum iyalpāṉatum eḷitāṉatum makaramātaliṉ, mutalāvatu tōṉṟiya cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu makaramey yīṟṟatē. atu piṉṉarp piṟa meyyīṟṟatākat tirintum, iṟutiyilum iṭaiyilum vēṟeḻuttup peṟṟu virintum, ēṉai yīṟukaḷōṭu kūṭiyum, palvēṟu taṉiyīṟukaḷaiyum kūṭṭīṟukaḷaiyum piṟappit tuḷḷatu.
am ikkālattil taṉiyīṟu; mutaṟkālattil kūṭṭīṟu (a+m).
am-aṉ-al. e-ṭu: tiṟam-tiṟaṉ-tiṟal.
uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya īṟukaḷellām coṟkaḷum coṟ citaivukaḷumē yātaliṉ, attakai īṟu peṟṟa col lel lām mutaṟ kālattil iru collākavē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: uṇā (uṇ+ā) = uṇṇum atu, uṇṇum poruḷ.
meyyīṟṟu muccuṭṭaṭi yīṟukaḷ

eṭuttukkāṭṭillaṉa iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
mutaṟkālattil, tamiḻil ṟaṉṉakaram tōṉṟavillai; tannakaramē yiruntatu. akkālattuc coṟkaḷē verin, porun eṉpavai. itu piṉṉar neṭuṅkaṇakkil viḷakkap peṟum.
la ḷa ḻa mūṉṟum orē kālattut tōṉṟavillai. la muṉṉum   ḷa iṭaiyum, ḻa piṉṉum tōṉṟiṉa. ātalāl, allīṟu piṉṉar muṟai yē aḷ. aḻ eṉat tirintatu.
lakara meyyīṟu rakara meyyīṟākat tiriyum.

lakaram takaramākavum tiriyum. āyiṉ, valliṉa mey tamiḻil īṟā kāmaiyāl ukaram ēṟappeṟum.
e-ṭu: mel-metu.

nālvakai yīṟukaḷ

iṅṅaṉamē ēṉaiyiru cuṭṭaṭikaṭkum oṭṭikkoḷka. eṭuttuk kāṭṭillaṉa iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.

aku, acu, aṭu, atu, apu, avu, aṟu; akku, accu, aṭṭu, attu, appu, aṟṟu ; aṇ, am, ar, al, aḻ, aḷ, aṉ eṉṉum īṟukaḷum, ivaṟṟiṟ kotta ikaravukaramutal vaṭivukaḷumē, iṉṟu muṟaiyē ku, cu, ṭu, tu, pu,vu, ṟu; kku, ccu, ṭṭu, ttu, ppu, ṟṟu; ṇ, m, r, l, ḻ, ḷ, ṉ eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivaiyellām uṇmaiyiṟ cuṭṭaṭiyiṉavākum.
iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa aṉ, aḷ, ar, atu, avai, a eṉpaṉa pālīṟukaḷalla; aṇ, aḻ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟu iyalpāṉa potu vīṟukaḷē.
cila iṭappeyarkaḷ cilaviṭattuc cuṭṭaṭiyīṟukaḷ pōl tōṉṟum.  avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
e-ṭu	: 	cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu	:	akam-kaḻakam eṉpatiṟpōl
	 	oru peyarīṟu (aku+am).
		iṭappeyar	:	akam = maṉai, iṭam. kallakam = 						kalvic cālai (kaṟkumiṭam), 						kaṉmalai, kalluḷḷa iṭam.
		cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu	:	iṭam-ōr īṟu, e-ṭu: kaṭṭiṭam = maṉai.
		iṭappeyar	:	iṭam-oru peyarccol.
				e-ṭu:	iruppiṭam = irukkumiṭam. 						kaṭṭiṭam = kaṭṭukiṉṟa iṭam.
ōr īṟu pala vakaiyil tōṉṟalām.
e-ṭu	:	avu	:       	irā-ira-iravu, cel-celavu
		aṉ	:       	am-aṉ, al-aṉ.
       		antu	:	am-antu, atu-antu,attu-antu.
pala coṟkaḷil lakaram takaramākat tirivatālum, mel eṉṉuñ col metu eṉṟu tirintiruppatālum, atu eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭiyīṟu al eṉpataṉ tiripākavē koḷḷap paṭṭatu. aḵtu eṉpatu atu eṉat tokkateṉṟu koḷvataṟkiṭamuṇṭēṉum, atu eṉpataṉ vali iraṭṭiya vaṭivamāṉa attu eṉpatu, paḵtu eṉpatōṭoppa āyta miṭaiyiṭṭa maṟuvaṭivu koṇṭateṉ patē poruttamām.metu metteṉṟāyatupōl atu atteṉ ṟāyatu; patu patteṉṟāyatu. āytattai yaṭutta takaram, aḵṟiṇai, kaḵṟītu, paḵṟi, paḵṟuḷi eṉpavaṟṟiṟpōl ṟakaramākat tirivatē puṇarcciyilakkaṇa mātalāl, aḵtu paḵtu eṉpavaṟṟi luḷḷa āytam iyalpāṉa iṭaiyeḻut taṉṟeṉṟum, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa iṭaikkālat tirippeṉṟum aṟintu koḷka.
collīṟukaḷ tiriyumpōtu, meli valiyākalām; vali meli yākalām.
e-ṭu:  nīṅku-nīkku, pōkku-pōṅku.
palvakaik kuṟippuc coṟkaḷ
accam, viraivu, ōcai, oḷi, vaṇṇam, acaivu, ūṟu mutaliya palvēṟu poruḷpaṟṟiya kuṟippuc coṟkaḷ, ōroḻuṅku paṭṭa īṟukaḷaik koṇṭirāviṭiṉum, makkaḷ karuttait teḷivāyp pulappaṭuttaṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: 
accakkuṟippu	-	kapīr, tiṭukku, tuṇukku, verukku.
viraivukkuṟippu	-	avakku, kupīl-kupīr, carēl-carēr, tiṭum,			tiṭīr , paṭakku, pucukku, pocukku, vicukku, 			viruṭṭu, veṭukku.
ōcaikkuṟippu	-	kaṇīr, kiṟiccu, kīccu, caḷār, taṭāl-taṭār,
			turuṭṭu, puḷiccu, paṭār, molōr.
oḷikkuṟippu	-	paḷiccu, paḷīr, miṉukku.
vaṇṇakkuṟippu	-	civīr, cevēr, veḷēl-veḷēr.
acaivukkuṟippu	-	kiṇukku, kiṇukkaṭṭi, cavakku, 					totukkup putukku.
ūṟṟukkuṟippu	-	kaṭukku, cuṟukku, cuḷīr, parapara, potukku, 			noḷukku, metukku.
(7) valiyiraṭṭal
e-ṭu: paku-pakkam, pēcu-pēccu, neṭu-neṭṭai, karutu-karuttu, kuṟu-kuṟṟam, kuṟṟi. neṟu-neṟṟi.
eḻuttiraṭṭal (Gemination or Doubling of Consonants) eṉṉum collamaiti, āriya moḻik kuṭumpak kiḷaikaḷuḷ airōp pāviṉ  vaṭamēlaik kōṭiyiluḷḷatum, āṅkilattai uḷḷiṭ ṭatumāṉa, tiyūttāṉiyattiṟkē ciṟappāṉateṉṟu (i) riccārṭu māricu kūṟiyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
taṉikkuṟilaiyaṭutta mey uyirōṭu puṇarumpōtu iraṭṭu vatu, tamiḻukkum āṅkilattiṟkum potuvām.
e-ṭu: tamiḻ-col+aḻaku=collaḻaku, iṉ+icai= iṉṉicai
E. thin+er = thinner, sit+ing = sitting.
(8) pōli (Interchange of Letters)
oru collil, ōriṭattil, oru taṉiyeḻuttiṟku allatu iṇaiyeḻuttiṟkup pakaramāka(patilāka) muṟaiyē maṟṟoru taṉiyeḻuttu allatu iṇaiyeḻuttu vantu, poruḷ māṟātiruppatu pōliyām. pōliruppatu pōli. pōlutal-ottal. iṭam nōkki mutaṟpōli, iṭaippōli, kaṭaippōli eṉap pōli mūvakaippaṭum.
e-ṭu:	mutaṟpōli	:	nāyiṟu-ñāyiṟu, maṭali-vaṭali.
	iṭaippōli	:	kuṭavaṉ-kucavaṉ-kuyavaṉ,neyavu- 			necavu,aracu araicu, nīntu-nīñcu.
	kaṭaippōli	:	kaṭam-kaṭaṉ, kūtal-kūtar.
poruḷ māṟātirukkum pōliyāl, ceyyuḷil etukai yamaippiṟkaṉṟi moḻivaḷarccikkiṭamillai. āyiṉ, pōli muṟaiyait taḻuvip poruḷ vēṟupaṭum coṟkaḷ moḻiyai mika vaḷampaṭuttum.
e-ṭu:	tarukku-cerukku, paku-vaku, mīṟu-vīṟu, katali-kacaḷi,kuḻal-kuṭal , paḻam-payam-payaṉ, vāyil-vācal, mottai- moccai, iṭam -iṭaṉ, nāḷi-nāḻi, nāḷi-nāṭi.
ilakkaṇappōli (Metathesis)
oru taṉiccolliṉ eḻuttukaḷum oru kūṭṭuc colliṉ uṟuppukaḷ allatu uṟuppuccoṟkaḷum, muṉ piṉṉāka muṟaimāṟi  variṉ ilakkaṇappōli eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	taṉiccol	:	alari-araḷi, koppuḷam- 				pokkuḷam, catai-tacai, ñimiṟu-			miñiṟu, viciṟi- civiṟi.
	kūṭṭuccol	:	ilvāy-vāyil, ilmuṉṟu-				muṉṟil- muṟṟam, katuvāli-				kavutāri, tāṉaimuṉ-muṉṟāṉai.
(9) mūvakaic coṟkiṭakkai
taṉiccol, kūṭṭuccol, toṭarccol eṉac coṟ kiṭakkai mūvakaippaṭum. avai muṟaiyē oru collum iru collum iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pala collumākum .
e-ṭu:	nilam; taṇṇīr; ceṅkāl nārai, vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa 			pāṇṭiyaṉ.

aṭukkuttoṭar oṟṟiṭai mikkum tirintum tokkum varum.
e-ṭu:	palappala, paṟpala, meṉmēlum, nannāṉku, vevvēṟu. 	cila coṟkaḷ taṉiccoṟkum kūṭṭuccoṟkum potuvāyirukkum.
oru poruḷ tantu oru collāyum iruporuḷ tantu iru collāyum iruppatu potuccol.
aḷittēṉ = tantēṉ (orucol), vaṇṭuttēṉ (iru col).
ceyvāṉ = purivāṉ (orucol), ceyaṟkai maḻai (iru col).
toṭarccol vakaikaḷ
(1)	aṭukkukkiḷavi	e-ṭu:	kuṭukuṭukuṭu.
(2)	mīmicaiccol    	  ,,	mutupaḻantoṉ(moḻi).
(3)	aṭukkuttoṭar             	,,	tīttīttī.
(4)	mikavuccol                	,,	araiñāṇkayiṟu, peṇ 					piḷḷaip piḷḷai.
(5)	aṭaipeṟṟa col 	,,        	tūṅkeyileṟinta 						toṭittōṭcempiyaṉ, 					maturaikkaṇakkāyaṉār 					makaṉār nakkīrar.
(6)	kiḷaviyam                 	,,	tamiḻaṉ urimaipeṟavēṇṭiṉ

(10) poruṭkēṟṟa oliyamaippu
potuvāka, mellōcaic coṟkaḷ ceyaliṉ meṉmai yaiyum  vallōcaic coṟkaḷ ceyaliṉ vaṉmai yaiyum uṇarttum.
e-ṭu:	kuttutal = cataiyuḷḷa iṭattil muṭṭiyāl allatu kattiyāl tākkutal. kuṭṭutal = vaṉmaiyāṉa maṇṭaiyil muṭṭiyāl tākkutal.
	muttutal = mukattōṭu allatu vēṟuṟuppoṭu mukam mellap poruntutal. muṭṭutal = maṇṭai yoṭu maṇṭai iṭittal.
	arital = ciṟitāy naṟukkutal. aṟuttal =  peritāy  naṟukkutal. arivatu arivāḷ; aṟuppatu aṟuvāḷ. 
	kaṟittal = kaṉṟu iḷampullai metuvāyk  kaṭittal. kaṭittal = peritāṉa vilaṅku mutirnta payirai valitākak kaṭittal.
	caṉṉam = melliyatu, tiṇṇam = tiraṇṭatu.
	kuñcu = mellitāṉa iḷampaṟavai. kuccu =  vallitāṉa ciṟukōl.
	tiṇṭu = melliya pañcaṇai, tiṭṭu =vaṉṉilamāṉa ciṟumēṭu.
	karuppu = iyalpāṉa karuniṟam. kaṟuppu-ciṉattāl allatu mutircciyāl nērum iruṇṭa niṟam.
(11) marūu
cila taṉiccoṟkaḷum kūṭṭuccoṟkaḷum toṭarc coṟkaḷum,  vaḻakkāṟṟil maruvi, atāvatu ilakkaṇiyarāl oppukkoḷḷap peṟum vakaiyil kuṟukiyum tirintum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:
taṉiccol : elumpu-eṉpu, teṉku-teṅku, naṭuvaṇ-nāppaṇ, nerunal-nerunaṟṟu-nēṟṟu, pakuti-pāti, peyar-pēr, perumakaṉ- perumāṉ-pemmāṉ, pirāṉ; poḻutu-pōḻtu-pōtu, yāṉai-āṉai, viyar-vēr.
kūṭṭuccol : araiyuḻakku-āḻākku, cumaiyaṭai-cumaṭu-cummāṭu, cōḻa nāṭu - cōṇāṭu, teṅkaṅkāy-tēṅkāy, pukaviṭu-pukaṭṭu-pōṭṭu, puḻaikkai-pūḻkkai-pūṭkai, pōkaviṭu-pōkaṭu-pōṭu, varai yāṭu-varuṭai, māryāppu-mārāppu, mukakkūṭu-mukkāṭu.
toṭarccol
arumaruntaṉṉa-arumānta-arumanta.
karivalam vanta nallūr-karuvai.
pūṭkai, pōṭu eṉpaṉapōṉṟa coṟkaḷ, mūlanilaiyil kūṭṭuc collāyiṉum, marūu nilaiyil orucoṟpōl niṉṟu orē poruḷ tarutalāl, taṉiccollākavē koḷḷappaṭum.

teyvattaṉmai
	tiru 	tirumāl,  tirunāvukkaracu, 				tirukkuṟaḷ, tirunīṟu, 					    tiruvaraṅkam.
	tē 	tēvāram
tamiḻakattil piṟappōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya āriyak kulap piriviṉai ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, poṉṉiṟa vuyirikaḷ pārppāra eṉṟum karuniṟa vuyirikaḷ paṟai eṉṟum aṭaipeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu:	pārppāra nākam, paṟai nākam.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpaik kāṭṭum aṭaimoḻi, ap poruḷkaḷaic ciṟappākak kaiyāḷum vakuppāriṉ peyarākum.
e-ṭu: 	kuṟavaṉ pōkaṇi = periya pōkaṇi. araca  maruttuvam = aracarukkuc ceyyum vilakkūṇillā maruttuvam.
cila karuvippeyarkaḷiṉ aṭaimoḻi, ak karuviyāṟ ceyyap paṭum allatu ceppañ ceyyappaṭum poruḷiṉ peyarākum.
e-ṭu:	kōṇiyūci = periyavūci.

	kai	kaṉṉi-kaṉṉikai, kuṭi- kuṭikai- 			kuṭicai

			nilai-nilaiyam

			putukkuppuṟam
(14) puṇarcci
irucoṟ puṇarvatu puṇarcci. moḻi potumakkaḷāl ākkam peṟṟatākaliṉ, ataṉ puṇarcciyum avaratu amaippē. kavaṉittaṟiyappeṟātu ēṟkeṉavē yirunta coṟkūṭṭu neṟimuṟai kaḷaiyē, puṇarcci neṟimoḻikaḷ (vitikaḷ) eṉa eṭuttukkūṟiṉar ilakkaṇiyar. iyalpupuṇarcci, tiripupuṇarcci eṉap puṇarcci iruvakaippaṭum. oruvakai vēṟupāṭumiṉṟi iyalpāyiruppatu iyalpu puṇarcci; ētēṉumoru vakaiyil tirivatu tiripu puṇarcci. tirital- vēṟupaṭutal. atu tōṉṟal, tirital, keṭutal eṉa mūvakaip paṭum. muṉṉillāta eḻuttō acaiyō tōṉṟuvatu tōṉṟal; ōreḻuttu maṟṟōreḻuttāka māṟuvatu tirital; muṉṉuḷḷa eḻuttō acaiyō maṟaivatu keṭutal.
puṇarcciyiṟ putitāyt tōṉṟum acai, irucoṟ kaḷai allatu oru collaiyum oru colluṟuppaiyum cārntu niṉṟu iyaippatāl (icaippatāl), cāriyai eṉappaṭum.
iyalpu puṇarcci
cāttaṉ+vantāṉ = cāttaṉ vantāṉ
nalla+paiyaṉ = nalla paiyaṉ
pāl+iṉitu = pāliṉitu
tiripu puṇarcci
tōṉṟal (Insertion)
vāḻai+kāy = vāḻaikkāy
avarai+pantal = avaraippantal
kaḷā+paḻam = kaḷāppaḻam,kaḷāmpaḻam
pū+ceṭi = pūcceṭi (pūkkum ceṭi)
pū+ceṭi   = pūñceṭi(aḻakiya ceṭi)
paṇattai+koṭu = paṇattaik koṭu
ūrukku+pō = ūrukkuppō
vara+col = varaccol
i+nāḷ = innāḷ
veḷ+āṭu = veḷḷāṭu
nāṭu+āṇmai = nāṭṭāṇmai
ivai eḻuttut tōṉṟal.
cekkār+kuṭi = cekkārakkuṭi
puḷi+paḻam = puḷiyampaḻam
kaṇ+potti = kaṇṇāmpotti
ivai cāriyait toṉṟal.
tirital (Mutation)
nal+cey = naṉcey
veḷ+kalam = veṇkalam
cem+tāmarai = centāmarai
vem+nīr = vennīr
vēmpu+ilai = vēppilai
taṇ+nīr = taṇṇīr
uḷ+nākku = uṇṇākku
kal+tāḻai = kaṟṟāḻai
nal+nilam = naṉṉilam
uḷ+tu = uṇṭu, īṉ+tu = īṟṟu
ivai tirital.
keṭutal (Omission)
maram+vēr = maravēr
tolkāppiyam+nūl = tolkāppiya nūl
ivai keṭutal.
aṉṟu+kūli = aṟṟaikkūli
maṇ+kaṭṭi = maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi
ivai tōṉṟalum tiritalum.
paṭṭiṉam = piḷḷaiyār = paṭṭiṉattuppiḷḷaiyār
nakaram+āṉ = nakarattāṉ
ivai tōṉṟalum keṭutalum.
vēmpu+kāy = vēppaṅkāy
paṉai+tōppu = paṉantōppu
ivai tōṉṟalum tiritalum keṭutalum.
marūup puṇarcci
cila coṟkaḷ olineṟippaṭi oḻuṅkāyp puṇarātu maruvip puṇarum. ap puṇarcci marūup puṇarcciyām.  maruvutal, neṟi tiṟamputal. 
e-ṭu: 	ātaṉ+tā = (āntā)-āntai
	akam+kai = aṅkai.
	nāḻi+uri = nāḻuri-nāṭuri
	maka+kaḷ = makkaḷ
uḷḷaṅkai (uḷ+aṅkai) eṉpatu aṅkaiyiṉ naṭuppakuti. itil am eṉpatu cāriyaiyaṉṟu.
entai, tantai eṉpaṉa marūup puṇarcciyalla. ivai em+tā, tam+tā eṉap piriyum. tā eṉpatu tantaiyaik kuṟikkum peyarc col.
tā+tā = tātā-tātai (tantaiyiṉ tantai)-iyalpuppuṇarcci
tā+tā = tāttā (tantaiyiṉ tantai)-tiripuppuṇarcci
tātai eṉpatiṟ pōṉṟē, entai, tantai, āntai, pūntai    eṉpavaṟṟilum tai eṉpatu tā eṉpataṉ tiripākum.
o.nō:	empi(ṉ)	eṅkai	evvai	emmuṉ	entai
	numpi(ṉ)	nuṅkai	nuvvai	nummuṉ	nuntai
	tampi(ṉ)	taṅkai	tavvai	tammuṉ	tantai
empiṉ = emakkup piṉ piṟantāṉ. kai = taṅkai (piṅ.). eṅkai = emakkuc ciṟiyavaḷ. em + avvai = evvai. itil avvai eṉṉum aṉṉaiyiṉ peyar aṉṉai pōlum akkaiyaik kuṟittatu. ic col cila paḻañceyyuḷkaḷil taṅkaiyaik kuṟippatu, kātal paṟṟiya marapu vaḻuvamaitiyeṉa aṟika. eṉ tantai, eṉ tampi, eṉ taṅkai eṉpaṉavellām vaḻakkup paṟṟiya iṭavaḻuvamaitiyē.
kārkālam, vaṭakōṭu, kōvūrkiḻār, nāṭukiḻavōṉ eṉac ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum, kēṭukālam, pēṟukālam, viṟakutalaiyaṉ, viṟaku kāṭu, vaṭakarai, maḻaikālam eṉa ulaka vaḻakkilum, valimikātu vaḻaṅku vatālum; nāṉku, veṭku eṉṉum tiripu vaṭivukaḷuṭaṉ nālku, veḷku eṉṉum iyalpu vaṭivukaḷum vaḻaṅki varuvatālum; palku, palporuḷ, cilkāl, valci, vaḷpu eṉac cila coṟkaḷ eṉṟum iyalpākavē yiruppataṉālum; mutaṟkālattil tōṉṟal tiritaṟ puṇarcci attuṇaik kaṇṭippā yiruntatillai yeṉa uyttuṇaralām.
mūvakaip puṇarcciyum oliyicaivai maṭṭumaṉṟi, iru poruṭ kiṭaippaṭṭa neruṅkiya toṭarpaiyum uṇarttutal kāṇka.
(15) ōriyalamaippu (Uniformity of Words)
orutuṟaippaṭṭa coṟkaḷaiyellām, mutalō īṟō etukaiyōpaṟṟi ottavaṭivākkuvatu ōriyalamaippām.
oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu, toṇṭu (oṉpatu), pattu, nūṟu eṉṉum mutaṟkālattu eṇṇup peyarkaḷellām ukarattil iṟutal kāṇka.
teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku, mēṟku eṉṉum ticaip peyarkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē.
pētai, petumpai, maṅkai, maṭantai, arivai, terivai eṉṉum peṇpāṟ paruvap peyarkaḷ aikārattil iṟukiṉṟaṉa.
atō, anta, avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avai, ampar, aṅku, avaṇ, atōḷ-atōḷi.
itō,inta,ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, ivai, impar, iṅku, ivaṇ, itōḷ-itōḷi.
utō, unta, uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvai, umpar, uṅku, uvaṇ, utōḷ-utōḷi.
etō, enta, evaṉ, evaḷ, evar, etu, evai, empar, eṅku, evaṇ, etōḷ-etōḷi.
ittakaiya ōriyaloḻuṅkupaṭṭa cuṭṭu viṉāc coṟkaḷ vēṟem moḻiyilum illai. atā, antā, aṉṉā, aṉṉa mutaliya coṟkaṭkum iṅṅaṉamē oṭṭikkoḷka.
(16) oppumai yamaippu (Analogy)
oru colliṉ etukai vaṭivil iṉṉoru collai amaippatu oppumai yamaippām.
etirmaṟaic coṟkaḷ 
iyaṟkai eṉṉum collamaippaip piṉpaṟṟi, ceyal eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarōṭu kai yīṟu cērttuc ceyaṟkai eṉṟamaittatu oppumai yamaippē. ēṉai yeṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ varumāṟu:
aṟam, maṟam; iṟappu, piṟappu; itai (putukkollai), mutai (paḻaṅkollai); iṇaṅku, piṇaṅku; iṉpu(iṉpam), tuṉpu (tuṉpam); kuṟṟam, naṟṟam; ciṟu, peru; ciṟṟu, peṟṟu (periyatu); ciṉṉañciṟiya, peṉṉamperiya; tāy, cēy; teruḷ, maruḷ; nalam, polam; naṉcey, puṉcey; nāṭu, kāṭu; pēraṭi, cīṟaṭi; nāṭṭam, pāṭṭam; muntu, pintu.
iṉapporuṭ coṟkaḷ
nāṟṟicaik  kāṟṟuppeyarkaḷuḷ, vāṭai kōṭai eṉpavum koṇṭal teṉṟal eṉpavum oppumai yamaippām.
oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ
koṟṟam, veṟṟam.
toṭarpaṟṟa coṟkaḷ
viṭai-viṭalai, kaṭai-kaṭalai, toṭai-toṭalai, muṭai-muṭalai; ai-(aintu) añcu, pai-pañcu, mai-mañcu, nai-nañcu.
(17) nāṉilak karupporuṭ coṟkaḷ
nilaittiṇai (tāvaram), iyaṅkutiṇai (caṅkamam) eṉṉum iruvakuppuyirikaḷuḷ, cila pala ovvoru nilattiṟkum ciṟappāka uriyaṉavākum. muntiyal tamiḻar kuṟiñcinilattil vāḻntirunta pōtē, mūviṭac cuṭṭup peyar, viṉāp peyar, muṟaippeyar, vaṇṇamum vaṭivum aḷavum cuvaiyum ūṟumpaṟṟiya kuṇap peyar, iṉṟiyamaiyāta viṉaic coṟkaḷ mutaliya aṭippaṭait tamiḻc coṟkaḷ amaintu viṭṭaṉa. payir paccai, vilaṅku paṟavai, uṇavu vakai, toḻilmuṟai, karuvipaṭaikkalam, paḻakka vaḻakkam, koḷkai kōṭpāṭu, kuṭiyiruppiṭam mutaliya palvēṟu karup poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiya coṟkaḷum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa, ataṉpiṉ kuṟiñciyaṭutta mullai nilattiṟkup paraviya pōtu, an nilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karup poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiya coṟ kaḷ piṟantaṉa. ataṉpiṉ mullaiyaṭutta marutanilattiṟkup paraviyapōtu, an nilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karup poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ataṉpiṉ, marutattai yaṭutta neytalnilattiṟku maruta nakar mīṉpiṭiyāḷar kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta pōtu, an nilattiṟkuc ciṟappākavuriya karupporuḷkaḷpaṟṟiya coṟkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
kuṟiñciyum mullaiyum mutuvēṉilil vaṟaṇṭa nilaiyē pālaiyātaliṉ, an nilattup piṟkālat tuṇṭāṉa coṟka ḷellām toḻilum karuviyum paḻakkavaḻakkamum koḷkaiyum paṟṟiyaṉavē.
kuṟiñciyaṭuttum mullaiyaṭuttum neytalirukkavuṅ kūṭumā yiṉum, kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar kuṟiñciyiṉiṉṟu āṟṟaiyoṭ ṭiyē paṭippaṭiyākap paraviyatākat terivatāl, marutanilaikkup piṉpē neytal ceṉṟiruttal vēṇṭum. mutaṟkāla māntar ceyaṟkai nīrnilai amaikkat teriyātirunta kālattil, iyaṟkai nīrnilai yāṉa āṟṟaiyē nampiyiruntaṉar. iṟṟait tamiḻakattiṟ pōṉṟē paḻampāṇṭi nāṭāṉa kumarikkaṇṭattilum, peru malait toṭarkaḷ mēlkōṭiyilē iruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu paḵṟuḷiyum kumariyum pōṉṟa āṟṟai yaṭuttē makkaḷ paraviṉ, kuṟiñci mullai marutam neytal eṉṟa muṟaiyilēyē nilaṅkaḷai aṭaintirukka muṭiyum. in nilaimai iṟṟait tamiḻakat tiṟkum ēṟṟatātal kāṇka. muntiyal māntar munnilai nākarikattaiyum muṟaiyē aṭaitaṟ kēṟṟavāṟu, kuṟiñci mullai marutam ākiya munnilamum aṭuttaṭut tiruttal tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟpōl vēṟeṅ kaṇumillai.
veṟpaṉ, kuṟumpoṟaināṭaṉ, ūraṉ, tuṟaivaṉ, viṭalai eṉṉum aintiṇait taṉiyūrt talaivarāṭcikkup piṉ, palavūrk kiḻamai pūṇṭa vēḷiriṉ kuṟunila āṭci vantu, ataṉpiṉ cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyariṉ perunilai āṭci tōṉṟiyapōtu, tamiḻ aintiṇaic coṟkaḷum cērnta peruvaḷa moḻiyāyiṟṟu, marutanilattil nakarkaḷ tōṉṟiya nākarikam vaḷarntu palvēṟu toḻilkaḷum kalaikaḷum aṟiviyalkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭa piṉ, tamiḻ mēṉmēlum vaḷarntōṅkiṟṟu.
tamiḻ aintiṇai makkaṭkum potuvāyirukkum iḵ toṉṟē, tamiḻarellām ōriṉameṉṟum, toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭilēyē vāḻntu varupavareṉṟum, toḻil taḻuvātu piṟappoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa āriyak kulappiri viṉaiyāl tūya māperun tamiḻiṉam ciṉṉa piṉṉamākac citaivuṇṭateṉṟum, terivikkap pōtiya cāṉṟām.
2. poruṭṭirivu muṟaikaḷ (Semantic Changes)
(1) uvamaiyāku peyar
cila peyarkaḷ uvamai yaṭippaṭaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	māṅkāy-māṅkāypōṉṟa neñcāṅkulai
	takkaṭṭi-takkaṭṭikkāy allatu kaṉipōṉṟa paṭṭaik f£o(sty).
	erumai nākku-erumainākkup pōṉṟa mīṉ.
	kiḷimūkku-kiḷimūkku vaṭivāṉa māmpaḻam.
cila poruḷkaḷiṉ peyar, paṇpiyākupeyar muṟaiyil ap poruḷkaḷiṉ niṟattaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: cāmpal vāḻai, kākkaip piciṉ, pavaḻac cōḷam, kurutikkāntaḷ, poṟkoṉṟai, mayiṟkaḻuttuc cēlai. cila peyarkaḷ uvamait tokaiyāy amaintuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	ārālmī ṉavarai. kōḻikkāl taiyal, pāmpuk kaṟṟāḻai, vauvāl mīṉ.
cila peyarkaḷ uvamai yaṭippaṭaiyil amaintu, teḷivupaṟṟi īṟu peṟṟum tirintum uḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	āṉaikkompaṉ-oruvakaic campānel.
	kāṭaikkaṇṇi-kāṭaikkaṇ pōṉṟa oruvakaic ciṟu tavacam (tāṉiyam), ataṉ payir.
	kutiraivāli-kutiraivālpōṟ katirtaḷḷum oru vakaic ciṟu tavacap payirum at tavacamum.
	nuṇā-nuṇal (maṇal tavaḷai) pōṉṟa kāy kāykkum maravakai.
(2) mūvakait takuti vaḻakku
iṭakkaraṭakkal (Euphemism), maṅkalam (Euphemism), kuḻūuk kuṟi (Conventional terms) eṉṉum mūvakait takuti vaḻakku muṟai yilum, cila coṟkaḷum vaḻakkāṟukaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	iṭakkaraṭakkal	: 	oṉṟiṟku iraṇṭiṟkuppōtal, pavvī.
	maṅkalam	: 	koṭittaṭṭutal = pāmpu kaṭittal.
			perumpiṟitu = cāvu.
	kuḻūukkuṟi	:	centalai = araikkāl. 					karuntalai=kāl. taṅkāṉ = arai. 				arumpu = arici.
(3) ōriṉappaṭuttalum (Generalisation) vēṟiṉappaṭuttalum (Discrimination).
ōriṉappaṭuttal
palvēṟuvakaip poruḷkaḷai, oru potuttaṉmaipaṟṟi oru collāṟ kuṟittal ōriṉappaṭuttalām.
e-ṭu: 	māṭu-peṟṟamum (kāḷaiyum āvum) erumaiyum.
	māṉ-uḻai, pulvāy, navvi, pukar (puḷḷimāṉ), marai, varuṭai, kaṭamai, miḻā, kācaṟai, kavari, kutirai, mutaliyaṉa.
	tumpi-muṉtūmpu (proboscis) uṭaiya yāṉaiyum kuḷaviyum.
vilaṅku, paṟavai, maram, mīṉ eṉpaṉa mika virivāka ōriṉap paṭuttum coṟkaḷ.
	perunelli, arinelli, kīḻvāynelli eṉpavaṟṟai nelli yiṉappaṭuttiyatum ōriṉappaṭuttalē.
vēṟiṉappaṭuttal
orē iṉamāṉa allatu otta tōṟṟamuḷḷa poruḷkaḷai, nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭupaṟṟi vevvēṟu collāṟ kuṟittal vēṟiṉap paṭuttalām.
e-ṭu: 	ilai-ilai, tāḷ, tōkai, ōlai.
	nilaittiṇai-pūñcaṇam, pācam, pāci, kāḷāṉ, pul,  pūṇṭu, kōrai, payir, taṭṭai, koṭi, tūṟu, ceṭi, maram. nuḷampu-nuḷḷāṉ, ulaṅku.
	camaitta tavacavuṇavu-cōṟu, kañci, kūḻ, kaḷi.
iṉi, ciṟappuccoṟkaḷaṉṟip potuccoṟkaḷē vev vēṟaṭai peṟṟup poruḷkaḷai vēṟiṉappaṭuttalu muṇṭu.
e-ṭu:	ciṟṟeṟumpu, poṭiyeṟumpu, āyeṟumpu, cev veṟumpu, maḻai yeṟumpu,  maṇṭai yeṟumpu, piḷḷaiyār eṟumpu, kaṭṭeṟumpu.
(4) aivakaip poruḷtiripu
(1) vēṟupāṭu (Variation)
coṟkaḷ kuṟittaṟkuriya poruḷai viṭṭuviṭṭu vēṟoṉ ṟaik kuṟippatu vēṟupāṭām. atu uyarpu, iḻipu, tolliyalpu eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.

(4) ciṟappikkai (Specialisation)
pala poruḷkaṭkup potuvāṉa collai avaṟṟuḷ talai ciṟantataṟku āḷvatu ciṟappikkaiyām.

	allatu toṭṭilil

poruṭperukkamum poruḷiḻappum
oru karuttiṉiṉṟu vēṟoru karuttut tōṉṟuvatāl, potu vākac coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ iraṇṭum palavumākap perukuva tuṇṭu. ētēṉumoru ciṟappupporuḷ kuṟitta aṭaipeṟṟa collaip potupporuḷil vaḻaṅkiṉ, ac ciṟappup poruḷaṭai taṉ poruḷai iḻantuviṭum.
