(2) coṟṟoṭarākkam (tōrā. ki.mu. 25,000)
toṭariyam eṉṉum muṟṟuc coṟṟoṭar, ikkālattiṟ pōṉṟē mutaṟkālattilum (eḻuvāy payaṉilaiyeṉṉum) īruṟuppu kaḷaiyō, (avaṟṟoṭu ceyappaṭuporuḷum cērnta) mūvuṟuppu kaḷaiyō koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, avai īṟum iṭai nilaiyumaṟṟa pakāccoṟkaḷākavē yiruntirukkum. aṟi eṉpatu aṟivu eṉṟum, keṭu eṉpatu kēṭu eṉṟum, cey eṉpatu ceykai yeṉṟum, iṉṟum poruḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
aṟikoṉ ṟaṟiyā ṉeṉiṉum 		(kuṟaḷ. 638)
keṭuviṉṟu maṟaṅkeḻu cōḻar		(puṟam. 39:7)
kaḷiṟu kaḷampaṭutta peruñcey yāṭavar	(neṭu. 171)
aṭi, viḻi, taḷ, col mutaliya ettuṇaiyō viṉai yaṭikaḷ, iṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peyarākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. cila malaiyāḷap paḻamoḻikaḷil nal (nallu) eṉṉum paṇpaṭi naṉṟu eṉṟu poruḷ paṭukiṉṟatu. uṇṭu illai eṉpaṉavum, ivai pōṉṟa piṟavum, iṉṟum mukkālat tiṟkum potuvām. kuṟippu viṉaikaḷ muṉpiṉ varum coṟṟuṇaikoṇṭu ikkālattil oru kālattai uṇarttutal pōṉṟē, terinilai viṉaiyaṭikaḷum mutaṟkālattil uṇarttiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
paṇṭait tamiḻilakkiyamellām ceyyuḷ vaṭivākavē yiruppiṉum, potumakkaḷ pēccu eṉṟum urainaṭaiyē yeṉpatai maṟantu viṭal kūṭātu.
ii ceyyuḷ naṭai (tōrā. ki.mu. 15,000)
tamiḻa māntar nīṇṭa kālamāka urainaṭaiyilēyē pēcivanta piṉ, mati viḷakkattālum uṇarveḻucciyālum tūṇṭappaṭṭu teyvappaṟṟu, kātal, pōr, vāḻttu, katai, varalāṟu mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷpaṟṟi, eḷiyanaṭaiyil teṉpāṅkum cintum pōṉṟa icaippāṭṭukaḷum, akavalum veṇkalippāvum irāmappayyaṉ ammāṉaiyum pōṉṟa ceyyuḷkaḷum, iyaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṉar. mōṉaiyetukai yamaippum coṟcurukkamum poruṭceṟivum avar uḷḷattaik kavarntatiṉāl, palvēṟu poruḷpaṟṟiya paḻamoḻikaḷum ovvoṉṟā yeḻuntaṉa.
(4) nālvakai eḻuttu (tōrā. ki.mu. 12,000)
aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkaip pēccum paṇṭamāṟṟuraiyāṭṭum kātalaṟivippum, vāymoḻiyāka nikaḻttuvatu nērilaṉṟit tolaiviṟ kūṭāmaiyiṉ, nāḷaṭaivil nālvakaiyeḻuttu muṟaikaḷ oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāyt tōṉṟiṉa. avai,
(1) paṭaveḻuttu (Pictograph),
(2) karutteḻuttu (Ideograph),
(3) acaiyeḻuttu (Syllabary),
(4) oliyeḻuttu (Phonetic Characters)
eṉpaṉa. eḻuttu eṉpataṉ mutaṟporuḷ paṭam allatu ōviyam eṉpatē. eḻututal-varaital. iṉṟum paṭameḻututal eṉṉum vaḻakkiruttal kāṇka.
(5) eḻutappeṟṟa ilakkiyam (tōrā. ki.mu. 11,000)
oliyeḻuttut tōṉṟiyapiṉ katai, varalāṟu, kaṇakku, aṟam, araciyal, maṇaviṉpam, pōr, maruttuvam, kaṇiyam, icai,nāṭakam, camayam, pukaḻcci, vaḻuttu mutaliya palatuṟaikaḷ paṟṟip paṉuvalkaḷum eḻuntaṉa.
ilakku eṉpatu kuṟi allatu kuṟikkōḷ. makkaḷ vāḻkkai yiṉ ciṟanta kuṟikkōḷai eṭuttukkūṟi, avarait tiruttuvatum uyarttu vatumē paṉuvaliṉ allatu nūliṉ nōkkamātaliṉ, avaiyiraṇṭum ilakkiyam eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ilakku+iyam = ilakkiyam.
paṇṭai viṟpayiṟciyāḷar, muṟaiyē parupporuḷ, nuṇ poruḷ, cuḻalāp poruḷ, cuḻalporuḷ ākiya nālvakai ilakkiṉūṭu eytu payiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟaiyē,
peruvaṇmai ciṟunuṇmai calam niccalam	(vāraṇā. 56)
eṉṟu pāratam kūṟum. peruvaṇmai eṉpatu marattiṉ aṭi pōlvatu; ciṟu nuṇmai eṉpatu marattiṉ mēluḷḷa ciṟṟilai pōlvatu; calam niccalam eṉpaṉa marat tucciyil amaikkap peṟum tiripaṉṟiyum cuḻalāc cakkaramum pōlvaṉa.
ilaiyai ilakkeṉpatu iṉṟum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭu vaḻakkākum. ciṟunuṇmaikkup perumpālum ilaiyaiyē kuṟiyākak koṇṭamaiyāl, ilakku eṉṉum coṟkuk kuṟi eṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu.
(6) ilakkaṇam (tōrā. ki.mu. 10,000)
ilakkaṇamum, moḻiyiṉ allatu ilakkiyattiṉ ciṟanta amaippaiyum naṭaiyaiyum eṭuttukkūṟuvataiyē kuṟikkō ḷākak koṇṭamaiyiṉ, ap peyar peṟṟatu. ilakku+aṇam = ilakkaṇam.
uḷḷuṟai teyvam oḻintatai nilaṉeṉak
 koḷḷu meṉpa kuṟiyaṟin tōrē	(tol. 993)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil, kuṟi eṉṉuñ col ilakkaṇattaik kuṟittal kāṇka. ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉṉum irucollum orē mutaṉilaiyē. poruḷ vēṟupāṭupaṟṟi īṟu vēṟupaṭṭaṉa. ivaiyiraṇṭum mutaṉilai yiṉālum īṟṟiṉālum tūya teṉcollē yeṉṟu teḷika. ivai vaṭamoḻiyil muṟaiyē laṣya lakṣṇa eṉṟu tiriyum. āyiṉ, aṅku  ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷaik kuṟippavai vyākaraṇa, sāhitya eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷē. lakṣya lakṣaṇa eṉṉum coṟkaṭku mūlamāka vaṭa moḻiyil kāṭṭap peṟum lakṣ eṉṉum coṟku, ilakku(kuṟi) eṉpatē poruḷ. ākavē, ivviru vaṭacollum teṉcol liṉ tiripeṉpatu teritaru tēṟṟamām. mēlum, tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūl āriya varukaikku 8000 āṇṭu muṟpaṭṭa teṉṉum uṇmaiyum, itai valiyuṟuttuvatu kāṇka..
ilakkaṇattiṟku aṇaṅkam eṉṟoru peyar kuṟik kiṉṟatu cintāmaṇi nikaṇṭu(103). katiraivēṟpiḷḷai avarkaḷ tokutta tamiḻmoḻi yakarātiyil, añcaṇaṅkam-pañca vilakkaṇam. añcaṇaṅkiyam-pañcavilakkiyam eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum irucoṟkum, aṇaṅku eṉpatu mutaṉilaiyā yiruttal vēṇṭum. aṇaṅkutal-olittal.
mikaiyaṇaṅku meynniṟīi mīḷi maṟavar
 pukaiyaṇaṅkap pūmāri cinti	(potuviyaṟpaṭalam, 8)
eṉpatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai . aṇaṅku eṉpatu eḻuttaiyuṅ kuṟikkumāyiṉ, aṇaṅkam aṇaṅkiyam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ, muṟaiyē, ilakkaṇattaiyum ilakkiyattaiyuṅ kuṟippatu poruttamē. āyiṉ, ic coṟkaḷ tamiḻakattil eṉṟum vaḻakkiliruntatākat teriyavillai. ilakkiyam ilakkaṇam eṉpaṉavē toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuvaṉa.
eḻuttu-eḻuttoliyam (Phonemics)
tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ, (1) mutal, (2) cārpu eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. mutalāvatu iyalpākat tōṉṟiya eḻuttukaḷ mutaleḻuttukaḷ; piṉpu avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ cārpākat tirinta eḻuttukaḷ cārpeḻuttukaḷ. uyire ḻuttup paṉṉiraṇṭum meyyeḻuttup patiṉeṭṭumāka, mutaleḻuttukaḷ muppatām. cārpeḻuttukaḷ mūṉṟām. ākavē, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ allatu olikaḷ mottam muppattu mūṉṟām.
uyireḻuttukaḷ, kuṟilaintum neṭilēḻumāka iruvakaip paṭum. meyyeḻuttukaḷum, valliṉam āṟum melliṉam āṟum iṭaiyiṉam āṟumāka mūvakaippaṭum.
(1) mutaleḻuttukaḷ (Phonemes)
māntaṉ taṉ vāyait tiṟantavuṭaṉ iyalpāka olikkum oli akaram allatu ākāram. kuḻantai piṟantavuṭaṉ aḻum oliyum muḻaittal muṟaippaṭṭa akara ākāramē. ākavē, mutalāvatu māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya uyiroliyē; atu akaramē.
vāy tiṟavāta nilaiyil, mūkkuvaḷiyāl olikkum oli makaram. ākavē, meyyolikaḷuḷ mutalāvatu tōṉṟiyatu, itaḻ maṭṭum porutti eḷitāka volikkum makaramākum.
iṅṅaṉam mutaṉmutalākat tōṉṟiya akaramum makaramuñ cēriṉ, am eṉṉum acai piṟakkum. orukāl itiṉiṉṟu, amma eṉṉum muṟaippeyar kuḻavivaḷarp poliyāyt tōṉṟi yirukkalām. pāluṟuppaik kuṟikkum ammam eṉṉum col marumam eṉpataṉ tiripākum. marumam-mammam-ammam.
māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya moḻiyolikaḷuḷ, uyirel lām muṉpum meyyellām piṉpumākat tōṉṟavillai, uyirum meyyum oḻuṅkiṉṟi māṟimāṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa. am muṟaiyai iṉṟu kāṇpatu aritu. āyiṉum, uyiriṉattaiyum meyyiṉattai yum vēṟu pirittu ovvōri ṉattaiyuñ cērnta olikaḷ tōṉṟiya muṟaiyai oruvāṟu aṟiyalām.
uyir tōṉṟiya muṟai
mutal tōṉṟiyavai	:	neṭil-ā, ī, ū
iṭaittoṉṟiyavai	:	neṭil-ē, ō
		kuṟil-a, i, u
kaṭaittōṉṟiyavai	:	neṭil-ai, au
		kuṟil-e, o
uyirkaḷuḷ, neṭil muṉpum kuṟil piṉpum tōṉṟiṉa. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷvataṟku ētukkaḷ muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. mutalil tōṉṟiya mūvuyirum, muṟaiyē, cēymai yaṇmai muṉmaic cuṭṭukaḷākavē tōṉṟiṉa.
aṇmaic cuṭṭiṉum muṉmaic cuṭṭu pintiyatātalālum, caṟṟu mikunta muyaṟci vēṇṭutalāṉum, īkāram ūkārattiṟku muntiyatātal vēṇṭum.
ikara īkārattiṉ mōṉaiyāka ekara ēkāramum, ukara ūkārattiṉ mōṉaiyāka okara ōkāramum tōṉṟutalāṉum; koccai vaḻakkil iṭam eṉpatu eṭam eṉṟum, uṉakku eṉpatu oṉakku eṉṟum, ikaram ekaramākavum ukaram okaramākavum olippatālum, aṇmaic cuṭṭi liruntē ekara ēkāramum, muṉmaic cuṭṭiliruntē okara ōkāramum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
aḷapu vakaiyil, 
āī  ūē  aiō  aue(ṉ)ṉum
 appāl ēḻum
 īraḷa picaikkum neṭṭeḻut teṉpa
eṉṟum, amaippu vakaiyil,
akara ikaram aikāra mākum. 	(54)
akara ukaram aukāra mākum  		(55)
eṉṟum, tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl, akara ikaram cērntu aikāramum akaravukaram cērntu aukāramum puṇarolikaḷāyt (diphthongs) tōṉṟiṉaveṉa aṟiyalām. ikaram ukarattiṉum muntiya tātaliṉ, ikarak kūṟuḷḷa aikāram ukarak kūṟuḷḷa aukārattiṉum muntit tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum,
vaṭanūlār puṇarccit tiripaip paṟṟukkōṭākak koṇṭu, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷaiyum puṇarolikaḷākak koḷvar. ak koḷkai tamiḻukku ēṟkātu. muṟkūṟiyavāṟu, avai mōṉait tiripāy eḻuntavaiyē.
ōriru coṉmutalil varum akara ikaram aikāramākavum eḻutap peṟum.
e-ṭu: 	vaḷ-vay-vayir-vayiram-vairam.
ōriru coṉmutalil varum akara ukaram aukāramākavum eḻutappeṟum.
e-ṭu: 	katuvāli (= kuṟukiya vālaiyuṭaiya paṟavaiyiṉam)-kavutāri (ilakkaṇappōli)-kautāri.
iṉi, akarattaiyaṭutta yakarameyyum ellāviṭattum aikāra mākavē eḻutappeṟum.
akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
 aieṉ neṭuñciṉai meypeṟat tōṉṟum.  	(tol. 56)
e-ṭu: 	vaḷ (=kūrmai)-(vay)-vai = kūrmai.
	ḷakara mey yakara meyyākat tirivatu peruvaḻakku.
e-ṭu:	koḷ-kōḷ-kōy, toḷ-toy, noḷ-noy, piḷ-piy, māḷ-māy, veḷ-vey- veyyōṉ = virumpukiṉṟōṉ. vey-	veymmai-vemmai=viruppam.
vemmai vēṇṭal.		(tol. uri. 36)
yakara meyyīṟṟu okara mutaṟcol īreḻuttuc collā yiruntu akara mutalāy māṟiṉ, aikāra vaṭivu peṟum.
e-ṭu:	poḷ-poy (uṭṭuḷai)-(pay)-pai,
akarattai yaṭutta vakaramey, oru cila coṟkaḷil aukāra mākavum eḻutappeṟum.
e-ṭu: 	avviyam-auviyam, kavvu-kauvu, vavvāl - vauvāl
auvai eṉṉum col avvai eṉpataṉ tiripēyā yiṉum, oru pulattiyāriṉ iyaṟpeyarātaliṉ, aukāra mutalatākavē eḻutappaṭal vēṇṭum.
ammai-avvai-auvai.
av, avvāṟu, cavvu, tavvu mutaliya cila coṟkaḷil, akara maṭutta vakaramey aukāravīṟāka eḻutappaṭuvatē yillai. āyiṉ, ay eṉṉum vaṭivam ay eṉṉum acaiyaik kuṟittālaṉṟi, eḻuttuttamiḻil nikaḻtaṟku iṭamēyillai. itai nōkkip pōlum, 
`akarat timpar vakarap puḷḷiyum
 aueṉ neṭuñciṉai meypeṟat tōṉṟum'
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟātuviṭṭatu!
uyireḻuttukaḷuḷ ai, au iraṇṭum puṇaroliyaṉkaḷ (diphthongs or compound phonemes); ēṉaiya taṉiyoliyaṉkaḷ (monoph - thongs or simple phonemes).
uyir tōṉṟiya vakaikaḷ
(1) aṅkāttal (vāytiṟattal) - a, ā
(2) cuṭṭal - ā, ī, ū
(3) mōṉaittiripu - i-e, u-o
(4) uṇarcciyoli - ai!
(5) vāycceykai - av-au
(6) kuṟukkam - ā-a, ē-e
(7) oppoli - ū (kū)
aṅkāttal eṉpatu maṟṟellā vakaikaṭkum aṭip paṭait tuṇaiyēyaṉṟi, taṉivakaiyaṉṟu.
mey tōṉṟiya muṟai
ma
pa, ka, ṅa
la, ra, ta, na
ca, ña, ya, va
ṭa, ṇa, ḷa, ḻa
ṟa, ṉa
kuṟippu: 
(1)	uyirum meyyum tōṉṟiya muṟaiyāka iṅkuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷa īroḻuṅkum, tōrāya (uttēca) muṟaiyē yaṉṟi, immiyum vaḻuvāta aṟiviyaliṉ pāṟpaṭṭa tulliya muṟaiyalla.
(2)	uyirum meyyum vevvēṟākak kāṭṭappaṭṭirup piṉum, uyirellān  tōṉṟiyapiṉ mey tōṉṟiṉa veṉṟākātu. iruvakaiyum ovvoṉṟāy allatu oṉṟum palavumāy māṟimāṟiyē tōṉṟiṉa.
	mutalil ākāravuyirum ataiyaṭuttu makarameyyum tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
yakaram tōṉṟumuṉ īkāra ikaramum, vakaram tōṉṟu muṉ ūkāra ukaramum, tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum.
ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa tōṉṟumuṉ ellā vuyirkaḷum tōṉṟi yirukkalām.
	(3) melliṉa meykaḷuḷ, makaram oṉṟē taṉiyē  tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum. ēṉaiyavellām ṅka, ñca, ṇṭa, nta, ṉṟa eṉat taṉtaṉ valliṉameyyuṭaṉ iṇaintē tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum.
kuḻavikaḷiṉ toṇṭaiyil ṅka eṉṉum oli piṟattal kāṇka.
valliṉameykaḷ taṉittaṉi tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum.
(4) 	māntaṉ allatu muntiyal tamiḻaṉ pēciya iyaṟkai moḻi, uyir, mey eṉṉum pakuppuṇarcciyiṉṟi, ōracaiyum palavacai yumākiya olikaḷaiyum ōcaikaḷai yumē koṇṭiruntatu.
meytōṉṟiya vakaikaḷ
(1) oppoli	-	k (kākkā), ṇ(kiṇ).
(2) vāycceykai	-	v (av), m (am).
(3) iṉattōṉṟal	-	ṅ (ṅka), ṉ(ṉṟa).
(4) puṇarcci	-	ñ (pū+cōlai=pūñcōlai)
(5) tiripu	-	n (porum-porun), r(l-r)
(6) mutircci	-	ḻ (ḷa-ḻa), ṟ (ra-ṟa).
(7) iyaṟkai	-	t, p.
aḷavu (māttirai)
eḻuttukaḷiṉ oliyaḷavu aḷapu allatu māttirai yeṉappaṭum. māttal - aḷattal. itu oru vaḻakkaṟṟa viṉai. māttiram eṉṉum collum itaṉaṭip piṟantatē. mā eṉṉum aḷavuppeyar mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyarām.
ōr imai allatu noṭiyaḷavu oru māttirai. uyirāyiṉum uyirmeyyāyiṉum, kuṟilukku oṉṟum neṭilukku iraṇṭum māttiraiyām. uyirmeyyil uyirum meyyum ākiya īreḻut tiruppiṉum, am meykku māttiraiyillai. taṉimeykku māttirai arai.
ai, au eṉṉum iruneṭilum taṉittu nillātum aḷapeṭukkātum colluṟuppāy variṉ, oṉṟarai māttirai yāyk kuṟuki yolikkum. aikāram colliṭaiyil oru māttirai yākavuṅ kuṟukum.
(2) cārpeḻuttukaḷ
uyirum meyyum ākiya mutaleḻuttukaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṉ cārpiṉāl tōṉṟuvaṉa cārpeḻuttukaḷ. cārtal - oṉṟai yoṉṟu aṭuttal. uyiriṉattaic cērntavai iraṇṭum mey yiṉattaic cērntatu oṉṟum āka, cārpeḻuttukaḷ mottam mūṉṟām. avai kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉpaṉa vākum.
tolkāppiyar kūṟiyavāṟu im mūṉṟē cārpeḻuttukaḷ. naṉṉūlār uyirmeyyaiyum vēṟu cila eḻuttuk kuṟukkaṅ kaḷaiyum aḷapeṭaiyaiyum cērttut tavaṟākap patteṉa virittuviṭṭār. uyirmey uyirum meyyum cērnta kūṭṭeḻut tēyaṉṟi vēṟeḻut tākātu. eḻuttuk kuṟukkaṅkaḷai yellām cārpeḻutteṉṟu koḷḷiṉ, āytakkuṟukkattaic cārpiṟ cārpeṉṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. aḷapeṭai eṉpatu eḻut toli nīṭṭamēyaṉṟit taṉiyeḻuttākātu. ivvuṇmai kaḷai yellām nōkkātu, cār peḻuttut tokaiyaip perukki yataṟku, māṇavarai mayakkutalaṉṟi vēṟoru payaṉumiṉṟām.
kuṟṟiyalikaram
araiyaḷapāyk kuṟukiyolikkum ikaram kuṟṟiyalikaram. atu mūvakaiyil tōṉṟum.
(i)	yakarattōṭu puṇarnta kuṟṟiyalukarat tiripu.
	e-ṭu:    varaku + yātu = varakiyātu.
(ii)	puṇarcciyāl amainta miyā eṉṉum muṉṉilaiyacaic col. 
	kēḷum + aiyā = kēḷumaiyā-kēḷumiyā- kēṇmiyā. o.nō: maruḷum-maruṇm.
(iii)	melliṉa iṭaiyiṉa meyyīṟu yakarattōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcci viḷaivu.
	maṇ+yāṉai = maṇṇiyāṉai
	vēḷ+yāvaṉ = vēḷiyāvaṉ
	ivvakaip puṇarcci ellārkkum uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu.
kuṟṟiyalukaram
taṉineṭiṟkum iru allatu palaveḻuttiṟkum piṉ, valliṉa meyyōṭu kūṭic collīṟāy varum ukaram, itaḻ kuviyātu, ikarattiṟkum ukarattiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭu, arai māttiraiyāyk kuṟukiyolikkum atu kuṟṟiyalukaram.
e-ṭu:	māṭu, cekku, paruppu, piṇṇākku, vantataṟku.
āytam
taṉiccollilum puṇarccollilum, kuṟilukkum valliṉa uyirmeykkum iṭaiyil, nuṇṇiya kakaramāka olittu niṟkum eḻuttu āytamām. āytal-nuṇukutal.
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāaytal
 āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam   	(tol. 813)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka.
āytam piṉvarumāṟu nālvakaiyil tōṉṟum.
(i)	puṇarccit tiripu
  	colluṟuppup puṇarcci:
	e-ṭu	:	al+ku = alku-aḵku
			veḷ+ku = veḵku
alkutal = curuṅkutal. veḷkutal = virumputal. veḷ-veṇṭu(ācaippaṭu). veḷ-vey-veyyōṉ = virumpi yōṉ.
coṟpuṇarcci:
	e-ṭu	: 	pal+tuḷi = paḵṟuḷi
			kal+tītu = kaṟṟītu, kaḵṟītu
			muḷ+tītu = muṭṭītu, muḵṭītu
			av+kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya
(ii)	taṉiccol tirippu
	e-ṭu	: 	attu-aḵtu, pattu-paḵtu
			pakuti-paḵti
(iii)	cāriyaip puṇarppu
	e-ṭu	: 	aḵkāṉ, maḵkāṉ
(iv)	iṭaiccerukal (oṟṟilvaḻi yoṟṟu)
	e-ṭu	:	ilaku-ilaḵku, vilaku-vilaḵku
ivvakai ceyyuṭkē yuriyatām.
āytam ōruyirōṭuṅ kūṭātu taṉittu niṟpatāl taṉinilai eṉṟum, puḷḷivaṭivāyiruppatāl puḷḷi eṉṟum, muppuḷḷi eṉṟum, taṉakkuppiṉ varum valliṉa meyyai melivippatāl nalipu eṉṟum  peyarpeṟum.
pattu, paḵtu; kaṟṟītu, kaḵṟītu eṉṉum colliṇaikaḷai olittuk kāṇka.
iyaṉmoḻiyākiya tamiḻukkuriya āytam, tirimoḻikaṭ kuriya polivolikaḷaip (Voiced Sounds) piṟappikkum eṉṉum kūṟṟu, tiripuṇarcciyiṉ viḷaiveṉa aṟika. āytavoliyiṉ kakaraviṉat taṉmaiyai aḵkēṉam, maḵkāṉ eṉṉum cāriyaip puṇarppālum kaṇṭukoḷka.
cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟaṉuḷ kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalu karamum tām niṟkumiṭattu vēṟoṉṟaṟkum iṭantarāmaiyāl, av viraṇṭaiyum maṟuvoliyaṉkaḷ (Allophones) eṉṉalām. āytam attuṇai oḻuṅkum yāppuṟavum peṟāmaiyiṉ maṟuvoliya ṉākātu.
eṇ
uṇmaiyil, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ mutaleṉappaṭum muppatē. ataṉālēyē,
eḻutteṉap paṭupa
 akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy
 muppaḵ teṉpa
 cārntuvaraṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē		(tol. 1)
eṉat tolkāppiyar, nūṉmarapu eṉṉum mutal iyalil, muntu nūlār moḻintavāṟu mutaleḻuttiṟkē ciṟappuk koṭuttuk kūṟiṉār.
mūṉṟuṟaḻnta patiṟṟeḻuttāṉ muḻuvatumāy uṉakkiṉitāyt
 tōṉṟiṭumat tamiḻ 			(kāñci. taḻuva. 244)
eṉṟu civañāṉa muṉivar kūṟiyatuṅ kāṇka. 
uyir paṉṉiraṇṭum mey patiṉeṭṭumākiya muppateḻuttu kaḷai maṭṭum koṇṭa tokutikkuk kuṟuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, ataṉoṭu 216 uyirmeyyeḻuttuṅkoṇṭa tokutikku neṭuṅ kaṇakku eṉṟum peyar. paṉṉīruyirum patiṉeṇ meyyoṭuṅ kūṭa, uyirmey mottam irunūṟṟup patiṉāṟām.
ellā moḻikaḷilum uyirmeykaḷ kalantutāṉ olik kiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟkuk kūṭṭu vaṭivam mutaṉmutal amait tavar tamiḻarē. tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟiyē vaṭamoḻi yilum piṟa intiya moḻikaḷilum uyirmey vaṭivukaḷ amaintaṉa. iṉṉum mēlai āriyattilum cēmiyattilum uyirum meyyum taṉittaṉi niṟuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. amerikku, cappāṉ mutaliya orucila moḻikaḷil maṭṭum, taṉi varikkum kūṭṭu varikkum iṭaippaṭṭa acaivarikaḷ (Syllabaries) ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaiyum kālattāṟ piṟpaṭṭavaiyē.
āytam uyirēṟappeṟāta meyyāyiṉum, ataṟkut taṉi vaṭiva muṇmaipaṟṟi ataiyum ōr eḻuttākak koḷḷiṉ, tamiḻeḻuttukaḷ mottam irunūṟṟu nāṟpattēḻām.
peyar
cevippulaṉāṉa moḻiyolikaḷ kaṭpulaṉāṉa vari vaṭivil eḻutap peṟṟataṉāl, eḻutteṉap peyarpeṟṟaṉa. eḻuttu eṉpatu varivaṭivaik kuṟippiṉ toḻilākupeyar; olivaṭivaik kuṟippiṉ kuṟiyākupeyar.
viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
 muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum		(tol. 1594)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar kūṟiyuḷḷapaṭi, tamiḻil mutalilakkaṇa nūliyaṟṟiyavaṉ, muṟṟat tuṟantavaṉum meypporuḷaṟivu nirampiya vaṉumāṉa muṉivaṉāvāṉ.
muṉital = (ulaka vāḻvai) veṟuttal, veṟuttut tuṟattal. muṉi, muṉai eṉṉum iraṇṭum orē colliṉ vēṟupaṭṭa vaṭivamām. muṉintavaṉ muṉivaṉ; muṉaintavaṉ muṉaivaṉ.
muṉaivumuṉi vākum. 	(tol. col. 386)
iṉṟīm pālai muṉaiyiṉ	(perumpāṇ. 180)
ulakiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ ellām, uyir, mey, uyirmey eṉa mūvakai. uyir tāṉē iyaṅkum kāṟṟu vaṭiviṉatu; mey uyiriṉ utaviyiṉṟi iyaṅkāta uṭampu allatu kaṉapporuḷ; uyirmey uyiroṭu kūṭiya uṭampu. pirāṇi (pirāṇaṉai yuṭaiyatu)  eṉṟa vaṭacol tamiḻil vaḻakkūṉṟiyapiṉ, ataṟku nērāṉa uyirmey eṉṉum tamiḻccol vaḻakkaṟṟuppōṉatu ṭaṉ, taṉ poruḷaiyum iḻantatu. tamiḻil mutaṉūl kaṇṭa muṉaivaṉ, mūvakai yeḻuttukaḷum oliyiyakkattil mūvakaip poruḷkaḷai ottiruppatu kaṇṭu, ap poruḷkaḷiṉ peyar kaḷaiyē av veḻuttukaṭkum uvamaiyāku peyarāka iṭṭāṉ. tāṉē olikkum eḻuttu uyireḻuttu; uyiroṭu kūṭiyallatu olikkāta eḻuttu meyyeḻuttu; uyirmeyyaip pōṉṟa eḻuttu uyirmeyyeḻuttu. ittakaiya amaippu vēṟem moḻiyilum kāṇpataṟkaritām.
kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭaiyiṉam eṉpaṉa veḷippaṭai.
uyir, āvi eṉpaṉavum; mey, uṭal, uṭampu, uṟuppu, oṟṟu, puḷḷi eṉpaṉavum oruporuṭ coṟkaḷ.
muṟaiyum piṟappum
tamiḻ eḻuttilakkaṇam paṉṉiraṇṭuḷ, muṟaiyum oṉṟām. itu mēlai moḻikaṭkillai. vaṭamoḻiyuṭpaṭa ēṉai yintiya moḻikaḷellām neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟaiyil tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟi yaṉavē.
uyir, muntit tōṉṟiyatupaṟṟiyum, tāṉāy olippatu paṟṟiyum, neṭuṅkaṇakkil muṉ vaikkappeṟṟatu. kuṟumai paṟṟik kuṟil muṉṉum, neṭumaipaṟṟi neṭil piṉṉum vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ā, ī, ū mūṉṟum, muṟaiyē cēymaic cuṭṭākavum aṇmaic cuṭṭākavum muṉmaic cuṭṭākavum iyalpākat tōṉṟiṉamaiyāl, piṟa vuyirkaḷukku muṉ vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, ākāram vāy tiṟanta maṭṭil olippatāl mutalilum, īkāram vāyiṉ piṉpakkattil aṭinā viḷimpu mēlvāyp pallaip poruntiyolippatāl iṭaiyilum, ūkāram vāyiṉ muṉ pakkattil itaḻ(utaṭu) kuvintolippatāl kaṭaiyilum vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. itiṉiṉṟu, vāyiṉ piṉpuṟattiliruntu muṉpuṟam nōkkiya varicaiyil tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷ amaikkappaṭṭatai aṟiyalām.
ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ muṟaiyē īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷiṉ mōṉait tiripātalāl, iyalpākat tōṉṟi mutal mūṉṟuyirkaṭkum piṉ    vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, ēkāram aṭināviḷimpu mēlvāyp pallaip poruntiyolippatāl muṉṉum, ōkāram itaḻ kuvintolippatāl piṉṉum, vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ai, au iraṇṭum muṟaiyē ai, au eṉṉum puṇaroli kaḷātalāl, aṭinā viḷimpu mēlvāyp pallaip porunti yolikkum aikāram ēkārattiṉ piṉṉum, itaḻ kuvintolikkum aukāram ōkārattiṉ piṉṉum vaikkap paṭṭaṉa.
āytam nuṇṇiya kakaramātalāl, kakarattiṉ muṉ vaikkap paṭṭatu. 
meykaḷuḷ valliṉamum melliṉamum etiriṉaṅkaḷātalāl, mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiyavaiyellām valiyum meliyumāka iv viraṇṭāy muṉ amaikkappeṟṟaṉa. iṭaiyiṉam avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vaikkappaṭṭatu. ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa nāṉkum piṟa meykaḷ oḻuṅku paṭuttap paṭṭataṟkup pintit tōṉṟiyamaiyāl, iṟutiyil vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
valliṉamelliṉa meykaḷuḷ, ka ṅa aṭiyaṇṇap piṟap piṉavum, ca ña iṭaiyaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, ṭa ṇa muṉṉaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum ta na pallaṇṇap piṟappiṉavum, pa ma itaḻakap piṟappiṉavum ātalāl, am muṟaiyē vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
makaramoḻinta melliṉa meykaḷellām taṉtaṉ valliṉa meykkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyamaiyālum, valiyolitta piṉ meli yolippatē muyaṟcik keḷimaiyātalālum, valiyum meliyum aṭuttaṭuttu niṟpatu olivēṟṟumaiyait teḷivāyk kāṭṭu vatālum, valimuṉṉum meli piṉṉumāka iṇaiyiṇaiyāy niṟuttappeṟṟaṉa veṉa aṟika.
iṭaiyiṉa voliyiṉ iṭaittaṉmai valloliyum melloliyum aṟinta piṉṉarē aṟiyak kiṭattaliṉ, valimeli kaḷiṉ piṉ iṭaiyiṉam vaikkappaṭṭatu. iṭaiyiṉa meykaḷuḷ, yakaram iṭaiyaṇṇattilum, rakaram muṉṉaṇṇattilum, lakaram pallaṇṇattilum, vakaram pallitaḻilum piṟappatāl, avai am muṟaiyē vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
kumarikkaṇṭattil neṭuṅkaṇakkut toṭaṅkiyapōtu, atil amaintirunta eḻuttukaḷ ittaṉaiyē. piṉṉar, nāḷaṭai vil ḷa, ḻa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum nāṉkum tōṉṟiṉa. ataṉālēyē avai iṟutiyil vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
an nāṉkaṉuḷ, ḷa ḻa muṉtōṉṟiyamaiyāl muṉṉum;         ṟa ṉa piṉtōṉṟiyamaiyāṟ piṉṉum vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ḷa, ḻa iraṇṭum muṉṉaṇṇa varuṭolikaḷāyiṉum (Linguals or Cerebrals), ḻakaram ḷakarattiṉum caṟṟup piṉṉiruntu varuṭap peṟuvatāl, muṉvaikkappeṟṟatu.
iṟutimey nāṉkum pintit tōṉṟiṉa veṉpataṟku ētukkaḷāvaṉa:
(1)	ḷa, ḻa iraṇṭum iṭaiyiṉattuḷḷum lakarattiṟku muṉṉum vaikkap peṟāmai.
(2)	ṟa, ṉa iraṇṭum valimeli yiṇaikaḷuḷ ta na iṇaikku muṉ vaikkap peṟāmai.
(3)	lakaram tiraṇṭu ḷakaramum ḷakaram tiraṇṭu ḻakaramum ātal.
e-ṭu: kal-karumai. kal+tu = kaḵṟu. kaḵṟeṉal= karumaik kuṟippu. kal-kāl=karumai. kāl kāḷ=karumai. kāḷ- kāḻ = karumai.
(4)	rakaram tiraṇṭu ṟakaram ātal.
	e-ṭu: ar-aṟu, oḷir-oḷiṟu, muri-muṟi.
(5)	ṉakaran tōṉṟumuṉ nakaramē colliṟutiyilum    vaḻaṅkiyamai.
	e-ṭu: verin, paḻunu
(6) tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyum ēṉaiyolikaḷiṉ meṉmaiyum.
ṟakaram tōṉṟiyapōtu ataṟkiṉamākavē ṉakaramum uṭaṉ tōṉṟiṟṟu. tannakaram ṟakarattiṟku iṉamākāmai kāṇka.
tamiḻiṉ toṉmai, teṉmai, muṉmai, meṉmai mutaliya taṉmaikaḷai aṟiyāmaiyāl, kālṭuvel uḷḷiṭṭa mēlai yārāycciyāḷar āriya ēmāṟṟai nampit tamiḻaic camaṟkiruta aṭippaṭaiyilāyntu, pēraṉ pāṭṭaṉaip peṟṟāṉ eṉṉum muṟaiyil, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku camaṟkirutattaip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉṟu muṭivu koṇṭuviṭṭaṉar. itaṉāl cila koṇṭāṉ mārum tamiḻp paṟṟaṟṟa tiraviṭat tamiḻp pulavarum ḻa, ḷa, ṟa, ṉa nāṉkum vaṭamoḻiyi lillāmaiyāl neṭuṅkaṇakkiṟutiyil vaikkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu, pitaṟṟalāyiṉar. vaṭamoḻiyiliṉ maiyē iṟutivaippiṟkuk karaṇiyamāyiṉ, vaṭamoḻi yilillāta ekara okaramum iṟutiyil vaikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭumē! aṅṅaṉa miṉmaiyiṉ, atu pōli yuraiyeṉa maṟukka.
uruvam
eḻuttu eṉṉum peyarē, tamiḻukku ilakkaṇan tōṉṟu muṉṉarē eḻuttiruntamaiyai uṇarttum. eḻutuvatu eḻuttu. eḻututal-varaital.
eḻutuṅkāṟ kōlkāṇāk kaṇṇēpōl  	 (kuṟaḷ. 1285)
iṉṉa palapala eḻuttunilai maṇṭapam		(paripā. 1953)
eḻuvutal eṉpatu icaikkaruviyiṉiṉṟu oliyeḻup putalaik kuṟikkumē yaṉṟi, eḻututalaik kuṟikkātu.
paṇṭait tamiḻeḻuttukaḷellām pirāmiyiṉ tiripāṉa vaṭṭeḻuttukaḷeṉṟum, kiranta veḻuttut teṉṉāṭṭil piṟ kālattil vaṭamoḻikkeṉ ṟēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭateṉṟum, potuvākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ, eḻuttuvaṭivampaṟṟiya cila tolkāppiya nūṟpākkaḷaiyum naṉṉūl nūṟpākkaḷaiyum, paṇṭai yēṭṭuc cuvaṭikaḷaiyum nōkkumiṭattu, ik karuttup poruttamuṭaiyatāyt tōṉṟavillai. iṟṟai vaḻakkiluḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttumuṟaiyē paṇṭaivaḻakkilum iruntateṉṟum, paṭṭayaṅkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa vaṭṭeḻuttu veṭ ṭeḻuttu eṉap peyarpeṟṟirukkalā meṉṟum, kirantaveḻuttu paṇṭait tamiḻeḻuttiṉ tiripē yeṉṟum, atu ēṭṭiṟkuriya āṇi yeḻutteṉṟum, koḷvatē poruttamākat tōṉṟu kiṉṟatu. itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷum ētukkaḷumāvaṉa:
(1) mikap paḻaimaiyāṉa tamiḻēṭṭilum iṟṟai yeḻuttu vaṭivēyiruttal.
(2) ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iṟṟaiyeḻuttu vaṭivait toṉṟu toṭṭateṉak kūṟal.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttumāṇ(ṭu)
 eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi		(naṉ. 98)

intiya nākarikam āriyarateṉṟum, tamiḻ ellā vakaiyilum vaṭamoḻiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyateṉṟum, mēlai yaṟiñar kuruṭṭut taṉamākak koṇṭirunta aṭippaṭait tavaṟukaḷē, tamiḻeḻuttup pirāmiyiṉiṉṟu tirintateṉṟu koḷḷaṟ kiṭantantaṉa.

ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ neṭilkaṭkup pintit tōṉṟi yamaiyāl, avaṟṟai vēṟupaṭuttikkāṭṭa avaṟṟiṉ mēṟ puḷḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷaip pēcuvōriṉ muṉṉōr, ekara okarak kuṟilkaḷ tamiḻil tōṉṟumuṉ kumarikkaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu vaṭakkē ceṉṟa tiṉālēyē, am moḻikaḷil iṉṟum ak kuṟilkaḷ illai. am moḻikaḷiṉ mūlamākiya pirākirutaṅkaḷai vēta āriyar pēciṉatiṉālēyē, avar vētattilum avai illai.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā veḻuttumāṇ(ṭu)
 eytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi	    	(naṉ. 98)
eṉṟu 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkattiṉatāṉa naṉṉūl  kūṟuvatāl, tamiḻ ēṭṭeḻuttu toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅki varuva teṉat tuṇiyalām. `āṇṭu' eṉpatu akkālattu (muṟkālattil) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatākum. ekara okara uyirum uyirmeyyum puḷḷipeṟṟa oṉṟē, paṇṭaiyeḻuttiṉ vēṟṟumai yeṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār pavaṇanti muṉivar. avar kālattilum avai puḷḷipeṟṟaṉaveṉiṉ, atai ēṉ vitantu kūṟal vēṇṭum? tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttum eṉpatilēyē atu aṭaṅkiviṭumē! ātalāl, atu uraiyaṉṟeṉka.
`eytum' eṉpatu vaḻakkam paṟṟiya kālavaḻuvamaiti.
piṟkālattil vēṟṟaracu vantuviṭṭataṉālum, tamiḻaik kākkum poṟuppuc centamiḻppulavar kaiyiliṉmaiyālum, ilakkaṇamaṟiyā māntar ēṭṭilum kallilum tappuntavaṟu māy eḻuti vantataiyē, vīramā muṉivar muṉṉai muṟaippaṭi tirut tiṉār eṉa aṟika.
māttirai
eḻuttukaṭku māttirai iṉṉiṉṉa aḷaveṉṟu kuṟikkap paṭṭiruppiṉum, kūppīṭu (viḷi), oppāri (pulampal), icaippāṭṭu, teruviṟpaṉai (paṇṭamāṟṟu) mutaliya ulakiyaṟ ceytikaḷil, avai cevippāṭu, tuyaram, iṉimai mutaliyaṉapaṟṟi, vēṇṭumaḷavu allatu iyaṉṟa aḷavu nīṇṭolikkum. atu aḷapeṭai eṉappaṭum. aḷapeṭuttal - māttirai kaṭantolittal. uyiraḷa peṭai, oṟṟaḷa peṭai eṉa aḷapeṭai iruvakai. oṟṟu - mey.
uyiraḷapeṭaiyai varivaṭiviṟ kuṟikkumpōtu neṭiliṉ piṉṉum kuṟiliṉ piṉṉum, iyalpāka vuriya aḷaviṟku mēṟpaṭṭa ovvoru māttiraikkum ovvoru muṟaiyāka, avvak kuṟil allatu iṉakkuṟilkaḷ vaṭivu cērtteḻutappaṭum. icaiyil aḷapeṭaik kuṟilkaḷ toṭarntum viṭṭum olikkum; piṟa vaṟṟil toṭarntē olikkum.
oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyāyiṉ, ellāviṭattum, kūṭiya ovvōr araimāttiraikkum oru muṟaiyāka avvam meyvaṭivē cērt teḻutappaṭum.
meyyeḻuttukaḷuḷ valliṉam aḷapeṭukkā.
icai kuṟaintaviṭattu atai niṟaikkac ceyyuḷilum aḷapeṭai nikaḻum.
ulakavaḻak kaḷapeṭaiyum ceyyuḷvaḻak kaḷapeṭaiyum piṉvarumāṟu peyar peṟum:

palukkoliyam (Phonetics)
uyireḻuttukaḷuḷ, mūkkoliyuyir allatu melliṉa vuyir (Nasal vowel) eṉa oṉṟu tamiḻil illavē yillai. avaṉ, vantēṉ, maram, pōṉōm mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa melliṉa meykaḷē, cōmpalāṟ cevvaiyāyp palukkap peṟāmal mūkkoliyaḷavāy olikkiṉṟaṉa. iv voliyai īṟṟayaluyir mēlēṟṟi, ataṉai mūkkoliyuyir eṉpatu tiripuṇarcciyē. avar, tāy, makaḷ, vantāl mutaliya coṟkaḷiṉ iṟutiyiluḷḷa iṭaiyiṉa mey, ōriṭattum mūkkoliyāy oliyāmaiyai olittuk kāṇka.
moḻimutal ukaram itaḻkuvittē olikkappeṟuvatāl, kuṟṟiyalukaram coṉmutaṟkaṇ varavē varātu. tiraviṭappulavar cilar karutukiṟapaṭi kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟumaṉṟu. olikkuṟukkattai allatu tiripaik kāṭṭutaṟkē, ataṉmītu puḷḷi yiṭappeṟṟatu.
itaṉaiyē,
meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal.	(104)
kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa 	(105)
eṉa māṭṭeṟintu kūṟiṉār tolkāppiyar. āyiṉ, itai yuṇarātu kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟeṉap piṟaḻak koṇṭaṉar āriyavaḻit tamiḻāciriyar cilar.
ai, au iraṇṭum tamittu niṟkumpōtu, ai, au eṉa irumāttiraiyē olikkum; colluṟuppāy varumpōtē ay, av eṉa oṉṟarai māttiraiyāyk kuṟukum.
āytam kakaravoliyaic cārnta teṉpatai, atu toṉṟu toṭṭu olikkappaṭṭuvarum muṟaimaiyiṉālum, ēṉaccāriyai ēṟkumpōtu aḵkēṉam eṉak kakarattaip paṟṟukkōṭākak koḷvatālum, kuṟuṅkaṇakkilum neṭuṅkaṇakkilum kakara meykku muṉ vaikkappaṭṭiruppatālum, paṇṭai neṭuṅ kaṇak kēṭṭuc cuvaṭiyil ka-ḵ eṉa varaiyappeṟṟamaiyālum aṟiyalām.
āytam varum coṟkaḷaiyellām āyntu pārppiṉ, atu perumpālum lakara ḷakarat tiripākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉ ṟatu. itai nōkkumiṭattu, kumarikkaṇṭattil ōriṭattu olittiri pākavō puṇarccit tiripākavō āytam tōṉṟiyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟu  terikiṉṟatu.

muntunilai yeḻuttukaḷ
tamiḻ ulakap perumoḻikaṭkuḷ mika muntit tōṉṟiya iyaṉmoḻiyātalāl, atiṟ piṟa moḻikaḷiṟpōl ellā eḻut tukaḷum coṉ mutaliṭaikaṭaiyākiya mūviṭattum varuvatillai.
mutaṉilai yeḻuttukaḷ
coṉmutalil uyirum uyirmeyyuntāṉ varum; āytamum meyyum varavē varā.
ṅa, ṭa, ṇa, ra, la, ḻa, ḷa, ṟa, ṉa eṉṉum oṉpatumeyyum uyirōṭukūṭi uyirmeyyākavum coṉmutal varā. uyirōṭu kūṭic coṉmutal varum meykaḷuḷḷum, kacatanapama eṉṉum āṟē paṉṉīruyiroṭuṅ kūṭivarum. ēṉaiyavaṟṟuḷ, ñakaram nāluyi roṭum, yakaram īruyiroṭum, vakaram eṭṭuyiroṭum kūṭivarum. avaiyāvaṉa:
ña, ñā, ñe, ño; ya (yavaṉar), yā; va, vā, vi, vī, ve, vē, vai, vau.
iṭainilai yeḻuttukaḷ
colliṭaiyil āytamum meyyum uyirmeyyumē varum; taṉiyuyir varātu. uyiruḷ, kuṟilmaṭṭum aḷapeṭaik kuṟiyāka varum.
k, c, t, p ākiya nāl valliṉa meykaṭkuppiṉ, taṉṉuyir meyyēyaṉṟi vēṟṟuyirmey varātu. itu uṭaṉilai meym mayakkam eṉappaṭum. piṟa meykaṭkuppiṉ piṟa vuyirmeykaḷ cilavum palavum varum. itu vēṟṟunilai meymmayakkam eṉap paṭum. mayakkam-kūṭṭam.
r, ḻ ākiya irumeyyum iraṭṭā; ēṉaiya iraṭṭum.
y, r, ḻ ākiya mummeykkuppiṉ taṉimey varalām; piṟavaṟṟiṉ piṉ varā.
e-ṭu: vāyppu, tērcci, cīḻkkai.
mra, mla; vra, vla eṉṉum kūṭṭukaḷ tamiḻil evvakaiyilum nikaḻā.
ṇñ, ṉña; ñya, ṇya, nya, mya, vya, ṉya; mva eṉṉuṅ kūṭṭukaḷ kūṭṭuccol liṭaiyilaṉṟi varā.

taṉiccolliṉ mutalil meyyum, iṭaiyiṟ cila meym mayakkamum, iṟutiyil valliṉa meyyum, varakkūṭāteṉṟu vilakkiyatu, paṇṭaittamiḻariṉ melloli vāyiyalpu paṟṟiyatu maṭṭumaṉṟu; etirkālat tamiḻariṉ vāḻnāḷ nīṭikkumāṟu, iyaṉṟavarai moḻiyolippu muyaṟciyaik kuṟaikkavēṇṭu meṉṉuṅ kuṟikkōḷuṅ koṇṭatākum.
viḻuttam (Accent)
oru colliṉ ōr acaiyil viḻum oliyuṟaippu viḻuttam eṉappaṭum. tamiḻil viḻuttam (1) iyalpu viḻuttam, (2) coṟ piriviḻuttam, (3) colvakai viḻuttam eṉa mūvakaippaṭum.
(1) iyalpu viḻuttam
iyalpu viḻuttam iyalpāka nikaḻvatu. atu eṉṟum colliṉ mutalil viḻum.
e-ṭu: koṟṟaṉ, vantāṉ.
oliyuṟaippu varavarak kuṟaiyumātalāl, coṉmutalil eṭuttalum (Acute), iṭaiyil nalitalum (Circumflex) iṟutiyiṟ paṭuttalum (Grave),   nikaḻum.
īruyirc collāyiṉ, eṭuttalum paṭuttalum maṭṭum nikaḻum.
e-ṭu: vantāṉ, makaṉ
(2) coṟpiri viḻuttam
itu oru kavarpaṭu poruṇmoḻiyil (palporuṭ coṟ ṟoṭaril), colvāṉ karuttiṟkēṟpa ētēṉum oru coṉ mutalil nikaḻum.
e-ṭu:	cempoṉpatiṉpalam	=	cempu oṉpatu palam 	cempoṉpatiṉ palam	=	cempoṉ patiṉpalam
	kuṉṟēṟāmā	=	kuṉṟēṟu āmā
	kuṉṟēṟāmā	=	kuṉṟēṟā mā
(3) colvakai viḻuttam
itu  colvakaikkēṟpac coṉ mutalilāvatu iṟuti yilāvatu nikaḻum. 
e-ṭu:  kaṭṭu 	   -      ēvalorumai viṉai
	kaṭṭu   	   -      mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyar, 					mutaṉilait toḻilāku peyar.
	tapu       =      (nī)cā - taṉviṉai
	tapu       =      cāvi (kol)- piṟaviṉai
cilar viḻuttattaik kuṟṟiyalukara voliyoṭu mayakki, kaṭṭu eṉṉum ēval viṉaiyīṟu muṟṟiyalukarameṉṟum, kaṭṭu eṉṉum mutaṉilait toḻiṟpeyarīṟu kuṟṟiyalukarameṉṟum, uraippar. viḻuttam vēṟu; muṟṟiyalukaram vēṟu. kuṟṟiyalukaram orupōtum itaḻkuvintu muṟṟiyalukaramākātu.
aḻuttam (Stress or Emphasis)
poruḷai valiyuṟuttaṟ poruṭṭu, oru collai yēṉum colluṟuppaiyēṉum aḻuttiyolippatu aḻuttamām.
e-ṭu: maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ tamiḻil māperu malai - 	peyarccol
maṟaimalai āṅkilattilum vaṭamoḻiyilum vallunar- 	(kuṟippu) viṉaiccol             
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ intiyum kaṟṟārkaḷ - iṭaiccol.
ii col
mūvakaiccol
mutaṟkālattil ilakkaṇavakaiccol peyar, viṉai, iṭai eṉa mūṉṟākavē yiruntaṉa. ituvē ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāṉa aṟiviyaṉmuṟaip pākupāṭām.
ilakkaṇavakaiccol mūṉṟum iyalpākat tōṉṟiya muṟai, potuvāka nōkkiṉ, iṭai, viṉai, peyar eṉpatākum.
uṇarcciyolikaḷum viḷiyolikaḷum kuṟippoli kaḷum, māntaṉ vāyil muntit tōṉṟiyavai yākum. ivai iṭaiccol eṉpatu teḷivu. cappu, tuppu, vikku, mukku, vimmu, tummu, ici, ciri mutaliya muntiyaṟ coṟkaḷ, viṉaiyākat tōṉṟip piṉpu peyaru māyiṉa.
cila iṭaiccoṟkaḷ viṉaiyākip piṉpu peyarākiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: ol (iṭai)-oli (peyarum viṉaiyum).
mutaṟkālattil ol eṉpatē viṉaiyākavumiruntatu. iṭai viṉai peyar eṉṉum im muṟai ellāc coṟkum taṉippaṭṭa nilaiyil ottataṉṟu. cila iṭaiccoṟkaḷ nēraṭiyāyp peyarāki viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	ella (ellā)-eluva-eluvaṉ (tōḻaṉ), eluvai (tōḻi).
ellā eṉṉum viḷiyoli iṉṟu ēla, ēlā eṉṟu vaṭivilum poruḷilum tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
cila coṟkaḷ mutalaṭiyilēyē peyarākavō viṉaiyāka vō tōṉṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu:	kākā-kākam, kākkā-kākkai (peyar). 
	kara-karai (viṉai).
iv virucollum āṅkilattil nērmāṟāy āḷappeṟu kiṉṟaṉa.
nām kākam karaikiṟatu eṉkiṟōm; āṅkilarō `The crow caws'  eṉkiṉṟaṉar.
cila viṉaiccoṟkaḷ peyariṉiṉṟē piṟakkiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu: 	uḷ-uṇ
	kaḷ-kaḷi, kātal-kātali, tēṉ-tēṉi
	uḷ = uḷḷiṭam, uṇṇutal = uḷḷiṭutal.
	kaḷittal = kaṭkuṭittal. tēṉittal-iṉittal.
nālvakaiccol
āriyar teṉṉāṭuvantu tamiḻ kaṟṟut tamiḻāciri yarāṉapiṉ, iyaṟcol, tiricol, ticaiccol eṉṉum moḻiyiyalvakaic coṟkaḷoṭu vaṭacollaiyum cērttukkoṇṭatu pōṉṟē, peyarccol, viṉaic col, iṭaiccol eṉṉum ilakkaṇa vakaic coṟkaḷoṭum uriccol eṉpataic cērttu, iruvakai       yilum coṟkaḷai nannāṉ kākkiṉar.
uriccol eṉpatu ceyyuṭkēyuriya col. col eṉpatu col vaṭivaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. āriyar ayalārāṉa tiṉāl, tamakkut teriyāta aruñcoṟkaḷait tokuttup poruḷ uraittuk koṇṭaṉar. itaṉ viḷakkam piṉṉark kāṇka.
(1) peyarccol
oru poruḷiṉ peyarē peyarccol. peyvatu peyar; peytal - iṭutal. peyariṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
peyarccol poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉai, kuṇam, toḻil eṉṉum aṟuporuḷpaṟṟi, aṟuvakaippaṭum; irutiṇaipaṟṟi, uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai, viravuttiṇai eṉa mūvakaippaṭum; mūviṭam paṟṟi, taṉmai, muṉṉilai, paṭarkkai eṉa mūvakaippaṭum. ciṉai =  uṟuppu.
irutiṇaikkum potuvāṉa peyar viravuttiṇaip peyar allatu viravuppeyar eṉappaṭum.
e-ṭu: āṇ, peṇ, tāy, piḷḷai.
taṉmai muṉṉilaippeyarkaḷ, eṇpaṟṟi orumai paṉmai eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. paṭarkkaip peyarkaḷ, pālpaṟṟi āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aivakaippaṭum.
1. mūviṭap pakarappeyar (Personal Pronouns)
taṉmaippeyar: 	orumai    paṉmai	iraṭṭaippaṉmai
mutalnilai	ēṉ	ēm	ēṅkaḷ	
2ām nilai	yāṉ	yām	yāṅkaḷ	
3ām nilai	nāṉ	nām	nāṅkaḷ
taṉmaip peyariṉ vēṟṟumai yaṭikaḷ
ēṉ	ēm	ēṅkaḷ
yāṉ  - eṉ	yām -em	yāṅkaḷ-eṅkaḷ   
nāṉ-naṉ	nām-nam	nāṅkaḷ-naṅkaḷ 
yāṅkaḷ eṉṉum taṉittaṉmaip paṉmaip peyar vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉatiṉāl, iṉṟu ataṉ iṭattil nāṅkaḷ eṉṉum uḷappāṭṭut taṉmaip paṉmaippeyar taṉ uḷappāṭṭup poruḷaiyiḻantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ē eṉpatu uyarcci kuṟittalāl, iyalpāka māntaṉuk kuḷḷa nāṉ eṉṉum cerukku allatu taṉṉalampaṟṟi, atai aṭiyākak koṇṭu taṉmaip peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyirukkalām. vaṭamoḻi yilakkaṇiyar taṉmaiyiṭattai uttama puruṭaṉ eṉṟum, āṅkilar ataṉai First- Person eṉṟum kuṟittataiyum nōkkuka.
ēpeṟ ṟākum.	(tol. uri. 7)
ekku, eṭṭu, eṇ, empu, evvu, eḻu, ēṇ, ēntu, ēr, ēṟu mutaliya coṟkaḷil ekara ēkāram uyarcci kuṟittal kāṇka.
iṉṟu vaḻakkilillāta vaṭivaṅkaḷellām paṇṭait tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉa aṟika.
muṉṉilaip peyar: orumai     paṉmai      iraṭṭaippaṉmai
mutalnilai	ūṉ)	(ūm)	(ūṅkaḷ)
2ām nilai	(nūṉ)	(nūm)	(nūṅkaḷ)
3ām nilai	nīṉ	nīm	nīṅkaḷ
4ām nilai	nī	nīyir(nī + ir)	nīṅkaḷ    
piṟaikkōṭṭiluḷḷavai vaḻakku vīḻntaṉa.
nī eṉpatu nīṉ eṉpataṉ kaṭaikkuṟai. atu ir īṟupeṟṟu nīyir eṉṟākip piṉpu nīvir eṉat tirintu, nīr eṉat tokkatu.
nīṉ, nīm eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷ iṉṟum nellai vaṭṭā rattil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
muṉṉilaip peyariṉ vēṟṟumai yaṭikaḷ
(ūṉ)-uṉ	ūm)-um	(ūṅkaḷ)-uṅkaḷ
(nūṉ)-(nuṉ) 	(nūm)-num	(nūṅkaḷ)-nuṅkaḷ
nīṉ-niṉ	nīm-(nim)	nīṅkaḷ-(niṅkaḷ)
iṉṟu 4ām nilai eḻuvāy vaṭivaṅkaṭkum mutalnilai vēṟṟumai yaṭikaḷē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
muṉṉilaip peyarkaḷ ū eṉṉum muṉmaic cuṭṭaṭiyāyt tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
mutaṟkālap paṭarkkaic cuṭṭuppeyar
orumai    	paṉmai      iraṭṭaippaṉmai
mutalnilai  (āṉ)    	(ām)              	(āṅkaḷ)
2ām nilai  tāṉ     	tām             	tāṅkaḷ
paṭarkkaip peyariṉ vēṟṟumaiyaṭikaḷ
tāṉ-taṉ tām-tam tāṅkaḷ-taṅkaḷ
ā eṉṉum cēymaic cuṭṭaṭiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantuḷḷa tāṉ, tām eṉṉum paṭarkkaip peyarkaḷ, avaṉ, avaḷ mutaliya aimpāṟ cuṭṭup peyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyapiṉ, taṟcuṭṭup peyarkaḷāy (Reflexive Pronouns) māṟiviṭṭaṉa. `Auto' eṉṉum kirēkkac collum, mutaliṟ cuṭṭup peyarāyiruntu piṉpu taṟcuṭṭup peyarāy māṟiyuḷḷamai iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.
ikkālap paṭarkkaic cuṭṭuppeyarkaḷ (Demonstrative Pronouns)  
cēymaic cuṭṭuppeyar  : avaṉ, avaḷ, avar, atu, avai
aṇmaic cuṭṭuppeyar  : ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar, itu, ivai
muṉmaic cuṭṭuppeyar  : uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvai
ivai muṉṉark kāṭṭiya av, iv, uv, al, il, ul eṉṉum cuṭṭaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavaiyākum.
al-atu, il-itu, ul-utu.
ukarac cuṭṭaṭip peyarkaḷ iṟṟait tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟaṉa; āyiṉ, yāḻppāṇattil vaḻaṅkuvatākat terikiṉṟatu.
avarkaḷ, ivarkaḷ, uvarkaḷ eṉpaṉa iraṭṭaip paṉmai vaṭi vaṅkaḷākum.
makkaḷ nākarikamaṭaintapiṉ, akavai aṟivu atikāram mutaliyavaṟṟil iḻintōṉaik kuṟikka orumaip peyaraiyum, ottōṉaik kuṟikkap paṉmaip peyaraiyum, uyarntōṉaik kuṟikka iraṭṭaip paṉmaippeyaraiyum, muṉṉilaiyilum paṭarkkaiyilum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. itu ulakavaḻakkēyaṉṟic ceyyuḷ vaḻakkaṉṟu.
taṉmaiyiṭattiṟku iraṭṭaippaṉmai vēṇṭā viṭiṉum,  oppumaipaṟṟi atilum amaintuḷḷatu.
mēṟkāṭṭiya mūviṭappeyarkaḷuḷ, aimpāṟ cuṭṭup peyar tavira ēṉaiya vellām, ṉakara  meyyīṟṟāl orumai yaiyum makara mey yīṟṟāṟ paṉmaiyaiyum, oḻuṅkāy uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. ivvīṟukaḷ tōṉṟiya vakai varumāṟu:
ollutal = poruntutal, oṉṟātal.
ol-oṉ = oṉṟu. oṉ-aṉ. o.nō: E one-an.
oṉ eṉṉuñ collē aṉ eṉṟu tirintu yāṉ (nāṉ), nīṉ, tāṉ, eṉṉum peyarkaḷil ṉakara meyyaḷavāka niṉṟu orumaiyuṇarttukiṉṟatu. aṉṉīṟu uyiroṭu puṇariṉ mutal keṭum.
ē+aṉ = ēṉ, ū-aṉ = ūṉ, ā+aṉ = āṉ
o-nō: maka+aṉ = makaṉ. kō+aṉ = kōṉ (kōvaṉ)
ummutal-kūṭutal. um eṉṉum viṉai iṉṟu vaḻakkaṟṟu, ataṉ aḷḷait tiripāṉa am eṉṉum collum, ataṉ vaḻiyaṭiyāṉa   kum eṉṉuñ collumē vaḻakkiluḷḷaṉa.

um-kum. kummutal = kūṭutal. kum-kummal. kum-kumpu-kumpal.
um eṉṉum col kūṭutalaik kuṟittataṉālēyē, atu eṇṇupporuḷ allatu kūṭṭupporuḷiṭaic col lākac koḷḷappaṭṭatu.

nāṉum varuvēṉ = nāṉkūṭa varuvēṉ, itai oru ciṟu piḷḷaiyum ceytuviṭum = itai oru ciṟupiḷḷaikūṭac ceytu viṭum.
iv vaḻakkukaḷil, ummaic collum kūṭaṟcollum muṟṟum poruḷottiruttal kāṇka.

ē+am = ēm, ū+am = ūm, ā+am = ām

aimpālīṟukaḷiṉ varalāṟṟaip piṉṉark kāṇka.
mūvakaic cuṭṭuppeyar muṟaiyilēyē e, ē, yā, eṉṉum viṉā vaṭikaḷiṉiṉṟu aimpāl viṉāppeyarkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.

3. paṭarkkaippeyarp pākupāṭukaḷ
taṉmaiyum muṉṉilaiyumaṉṟip paṭarvatu paṭarkkai. paṭartal = parantu cellutal.
(1) aṟuporuṭpeyar
ellāp poruḷkaḷum poruḷ, iṭam, kālam, ciṉai, kuṇam, toḻil eṉa aṟuvakaippaṭum. ātalāl avaṟṟiṉ peyarum aṟuvakaiyām.
poruḷ eṉṟatu. kāṭcipporuḷākavō karuttup poruḷākavō uḷḷa oru taṉipporuḷai.
ētēṉum oru poruḷiruppiṉ, atu irukka iṭamum kālamum vēṇṭum. poruḷkaḷ iyaṟkaiyāyiṉum ceyaṟ kaiyā yiṉum, perumpālum pala vuṟuppukaḷaiyuṭaiyaṉa. ovvoru poruṭkum cila taṉmaikaḷuṇṭu. at taṉmai kaḷē uṟuppaiyum uṭalaiyum tuṇaikkoṇṭu toḻilāka veḷippaṭum. iṅṅaṉam āyntu poruḷkaḷai āṟāka vakuttatum avaṟṟai muṟaip paṭuttiyatum, viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟivaṉ muṉaivaṉāṉa mutaṉūlāciriyaṉiṉ, meypporu ḷaṟivaiyum tarukkat tiṟaṉaiyum teḷḷitiṟ kāṭṭum.
kuṟiyīṭṭaip poṟuttamaṭṭil, kuṇam eṉiṉum paṇpu eṉiṉum okkum.
muttiṇaippeyar
uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai, av viraṇṭiṟkum potuvāṉa viravuttiṇai eṉat tiṇai mūvakaippaṭum.
e-ṭu: 	uyartiṇaippeyar-avaṉ, māntaṉ, murukaṉ
        	aḵṟiṇaippeyar -atu, maram, kal
      	viravuttiṇaippeyar-tāy, piḷḷai, āṇ, peṇ
	tiṇ-tiṇai = tiraṭci, tokuti, vakuppu.
aimpāṟpeyar
āṇpāl, peṇpāl, palarpāl eṉa uyartiṇaippāl mūṉṟum, oṉṟaṉpāl, palaviṉpāl eṉa aḵṟiṇaippāl iraṇṭum ākap pāl aintām.
paku-pakal-pāl = pirivu.
āṇpāl, peṇpāl eṉṉum iraṇṭum orumaip pālkaḷ. āṇpeṇ vēṟupāṭu aḵṟiṇai yuyirikaḷuḷḷum iruppiṉum, mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ ataṟku ilakkaṇattil iṭan taravillai. ēṉeṉiṉ, moḻiyai amaitta potu makkaḷē aṅṅaṉam amaittuviṭṭaṉar. ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ ilak kaṇam iyamputalē nūlāciriyaṉ ceyal.
kumarikkaṇṭap potumakkaḷ kūrmatiyum uyariya oḻukkamum uṭaiyarātaliṉ, nallataik kaṭaippiṭittut tīyatai nīkkum nākarikap paṇpāṭṭiṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta pakuttaṟivai aḷavaiyākak koṇṭu, ataiyuṭaiya makkaḷai uyartiṇai yeṉṟum, aḵtil lātavaṟṟai yellām uyiriruppiṉum illāviṭiṉum aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟum vakuttu, ataṟkēṟpa, makaṉ varukiṟāṉ, makaḷ varukiṟāḷ eṉṟu uyartiṇaiyiṟ pāl pirittum, kāḷai varukiṟatu, āvu varukiṟatu eṉṟu aḵṟiṇaiyiṟpāl pirikkātum, ellāp peyarc coṟkaḷaiyum viṉaiccoṟ kaḷaiyum amaittu viṭṭamai, eṉṟum tamiḻaṉ eṇṇi makiḻtaṟkum taṉ muṉṉōraippaṟṟip perumai pārāṭṭutaṟkum uriya toṉṟām. iṅṅaṉam vēṟem moḻiyilu millāta ciṟanta ilakkaṇavamaippu, iṟṟaip pula makkaḷiṉum aṟṟaip potu makkaḷ aṟivāṟṟalilum nākarikap paṇpāṭṭilum cālac ciṟantavareṉpataṟku takka moḻic cāṉṟām.

mutaṟkālattil, ovvoru tiṇainilattilum ovvoru vakuppārē vāḻntiruntaṉar. marutanilattil, nāḷaṭaivil ūr virivum toḻiṟperukkamum kalviyaṟivum nākarika vaḷarcciyum vaṇika muṉṉēṟṟamum pēraracāṭciyum matil cūḻnta mānakaramaip pum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, nīr vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭu, pēriyāṟukaḷ kaṭalōṭu kalakkum kayavāyil paṭṭiṉam eṉṉum tuṟainakarkaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟaṉa.

taṅkumiṭaṅkaḷ
pācaṟai allatu kaṭṭūr-pōrkkaḷattarukiṟ paṭaimaṟavarkka maitta ilaikkuṭil tokuti (War-camp).
pāḷaiyam-paṭaikaḷuṭaṉ potumakkaḷ taṅkiyiruppatum kaṭaitteruvuṭaṉ kūṭiyatumāṉa iṭam (Military Encampment). paṭaivīṭu-paṭaimaṟavar nilaiyāka vatiyum iṭam (Cantonment)

(3) kālap peyar
viṭiyaliliruntu oru nāḷ pappattu nāḻikai koṇṭa āṟu ciṟupoḻutākavum, mēḻa (cittirai) mātattiliruntu ōr āṇṭu ivviru mātaṅkoṇṭa āṟu perum poḻutākavum, vakukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ciṟupoḻutu
kālai, naṇpakal, eṟpāṭu (cāyuṅkālam), mālai, yāmam, vaikaṟai.
naṇpakal naṭuviṟku uccivēḷai, urumam eṉpaṉa tūya ulaka vaḻakkut tamiḻccoṟkaḷ. uccivēḷaikku muntiya pakal muṟpakal; pintiya pakal piṟpakal. 
perumpoḻutu
iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil, kār (maḻai), kūtar (kuḷir), muṉpaṉi,    piṉpaṉi. vēṉil eṉpatu kōṭai.
(4) ciṉaippeyar (Names of organs)
nilattiṇai: vēr, aṭi, kiḷai, ilai, pū, kāy, kaṉi mutaliyaṉa.
iyaṅkutiṇai: pātam, kāl, arai, vayiṟu, mārpu, kaḻuttu, talai mutaliyaṉa.
(5) kuṇappeyar (Abstract Noun)
vaṇṇam, vaṭivu, aḷavu, cuvai, ūṟu, nāṟṟam, iyal, ceyal eṉak kuṇam eṇvakaippaṭum.
vaṇṇam
veḷḷai, karuppu, nīlam, paccai, mañcaḷ, civappu, kattari (violet) eṉa vaṇṇam eḻuvakai.
nīr-nīl-nīlam = kaṭalnīriṉ niṟam. nīr = kaṭal. talaiciṟanta nīr
nilaiyum piṟa nīrnilaikaṭku mūla māyiruppatu kaṭalē.
nīrolit taṉṉa		(maturaik. 369)
kāṉak kōḻiyu nīrniṟak kākkaiyum                	(cilap. 10:116)
kaṭalvaṇṇaṉ = tirumāl.
nīḷ-nīr. nilam niṟpatu; nīr nīḷvatu. kattari (ppū) eṉpatu 	       ulaka vaḻakku. cilar atai īral niṟam eṉpar.
kāḷaivakaiyil pukar (kapilam), pullai, mayilai, karumayilai mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum; kōḻivakaiyil cāmpal, kamparici mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum; āṭaivakaiyil kempu, kāvi, nīrkkāvi, paḻukkāvi, (orange), tuvar, kaḷip pākku, veṅkāyam, māntuḷir, kiḷippaccai, pācippayaṟu, īyam, iḷanīlam, mayiṟkaḻuttu, vāṉavil, mukilvaṇṇam (mēkavarṇam) mutaliya niṟappeyarkaḷum; makkaḷ mēṉi vakaiyil taṅkam, pacalai, putuniṟam, mā mutaliya niṟap peyar kaḷum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar eḵkucceviyum kūrṅkaṇṇum nuṇmatiyum uṭaiyarātaliṉ, vaṇṇaṅkaḷaiyellām nuṭpa māy vakuttu avaṟṟiṟku vevvēṟu peyariṭṭiruntaṉar. paṇṭai yilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷvaṭivilēyē iruntati ṉālum, avaiyum aṭiyōṭu iṟantupaṭṭamaiyālum, pala vaṇṇap peyarkaḷum iṟantoḻintaṉa. iṉṟum mīṉpeyarkaḷil veṇ ṇiṟavakaikaḷaik kuṟikka veḷḷi, veḷḷai, veṇṇey, veḷuvai mutaliya coṟkaḷ āḷappeṟṟiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
vaṭivu
vaṭṭam, cataram, mukkōṇam mutaliya parappu vaṭivaṅkaḷum, uruṇṭai, uruḷai, kūmpu mutaliya kaṉavaṭivu kaḷum, palvakaiya  vaṭṭam, caturam eṉpavaiyum teṉ coṟkaḷē eṉpatu eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟu' eṉṉum nūlil viḷakkappeṟum.
aḷavu
eṇṇal, eṭuttal, mukattal, nīṭṭal, eṉa aḷavai nālvakai.
eṇṇal
eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ ciṟṟilakkam (kīḻvāyilakkam), pēri lakkam (mēlvāyilakkam) eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. ciṟṟilakkamum kīḻvāy, mēlvāy eṉa iru tiṟattatu.

toṇṭutalai yiṭṭa pattukkuṟai yeḻunūṟṟu 	(tol. 1358)
``toṭittirivaṉṉa toṇṭupaṭu tivaviṉ         		(malaipaṭu. 21)
toṇṭupaṭu tivaviṉ muṇṭaka nalyāḻ     (cilap. pa. 221. kuṟippurai)
toṇṭu eṉṉuñcol vaḻakkaṟṟup pōkavē, aḵtirunta oṉṟām iṭattiṟkut toṇpatu (oṉpatu) eṉṉum iraṇṭām iṭappeyarum, iraṇṭām iṭattiṟkut toṇṇūṟu eṉṉum mūṉṟām iṭappeyarum, mūṉṟām iṭattiṟkut toḷḷāyiram eṉṉum nālām iṭappeyarum vantu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. nālām iṭappeyarukku mēliṭaccol iṉmaiyāl, toṇpatu eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa oṉpatu eṉṉuñ colloṭu āyiram eṉpataic cērttu, oṉpatāyiram allatu oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉṟu collavēṇṭi yatāyiṟṟu.
oṉṟu mutal pattuvaraiyuḷḷa peyarkaḷellām taṉic collāyiruppataiyum, oṉpatu eṉpatu kūṭṭuccollāyum patu (pattu) eṉṟu muṭivatāyum iruppataiyum, nōkkuka.

vaṭuvāḻ ekkar maṇaliṉum palavē.	(puṟam. 55:17-21)
ilakkam	:	ilakku=kuṟi. ilakku-ilakkam = kuṟi, 	eṇkuṟi, 	eṇ, pēreṇ.
kōṭi	:	kuṭu-kuṭumi = ucci, talaiyucci, āṭavar talai mayirkkaṟṟai, paṟavaiccūṭṭu, makuṭam, māṭavucci, malaiyucci, nuṉi. kuṭu-koṭu. koṭumuṭi = malai yucci. koṭu-	kōṭi = nuṉi, muṉai, kaṭaici, ellai.
		kaṭaikōṭi eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. terukkōṭi viṟkōṭi eṉpaṉavum muṉaiyaik kuṟittal kāṇka. 	kōṭi kaṭaici yeṇṇāta lāl appeyar koṇṭatu.
tāmarai, kuvaḷai, neytal, āmpal mutaliya malarp peyarkaḷ ovvōr itaḻukkum; caṅkam (caṅku) eṉpatu ovvoru purikkum (vaḷaiviṟkum); oru peruntokaiyāka uṟaiyiṭal muṟaiyilum; nāṭu, vāraṇam, veḷḷam eṉpaṉa pēraḷavupaṟṟiyum; kaṇikam(nūṟukōṭi) eṉpatu kaṇippuppaṟṟiyum; palvēṟu aṭukkiya kōṭip peyarāyiṉa. eṭṭut tāmarai koṇṭatu oru nāṭu.
paratam = ilakkam kōṭik kōṭākōṭi. itu 1-ṉ piṉ 24 cuṉṉaṅkoṇṭatu. paratam eṉpatu kaṭal vaṇikattiṉ peyar.
paṭavaṉ-paravaṉ-paratavaṉ-paratavam-paratam. iṉi, parata kaṇṭam eṉiṉumām. parataṉ tiṅkaṭkulattaic cērnta oru paḻam pāṇṭiyaṉē.
